Financial Report Source 19

Document 002: 10月CPI再超预期，基本锁定12月美联储加息，升息幅度仍有不确定性

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月CPI再超预期，基本锁定12月美联储加息，升息幅度仍有不确定性.pdf
Report source: 浦银国际证券
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

从环比细分数据来看，10 月核心商品和住房 CPI 通胀率均略微下滑，
但运输、 医疗等服务价格仍有韧性， 核心通胀降温仍不顺畅（图表 1）。

 核心商品价格环比通胀率回落 0.09 个百分点到 0.16%， 二手车价
格转跌形成明显拖累， 高油价传导效应开始显现。其中，运输 CPI
环比通胀率部分因为二手车价格大跌而走低 0.77 个百分点到 -
0.38%。教育和通讯商品 CPI 环比通胀率亦收窄 0.37 个百分点到
0.5%，或受到计算机、外围设备 CPI 环比通胀率下滑的影响。这
或暗示着芯片价格暴涨引起的相关产业链价格大涨的最坏时期
已经过去。与此同时，8-9 月连续低迷的家具和医疗护理商品价格
10 月环比增速显著改善。家具价格的上升或与油价传导有关。娱
乐商品 CPI 环比通胀率略微下滑，但仍维持 0.71%的较高水平。
二手车涨价趋势的逆转表明，前期部分推升因素确有波动性，但
其他商品价格回升抵消了部分降温作用。核心商品尚未回到持续
通缩状态。

 住房 CPI 环比通胀率 10 月仅略微回落 0.09 个百分点到 0.3%，仍
是通胀重要来源。尽管涨幅有所收窄，住房仍贡献了超过三成的
当月核心 CPI 增幅， 反映高权重分项的通胀粘性仍强。其中，最为
关键的业主等价租金 CPI 环比通胀率 10 月仅小幅下滑 0.03 个百
分点到 0.32%。主要居所租金 CPI 环比通胀率微降至 0.2%。但考虑
到房价趋势，我们依然认为该分项有下降空间，后续需观察租金
和业主等价租金 CPI 环比通胀率能否连续放缓。外宿 CPI 环比通
胀率从 9 月的 2.04%下滑到 0.64%。

 排除了住房的核心服务 CPI（超级核心服务 CPI）环比通胀率 10
月反弹 0.05 个百分点到 0.39%，压力仍较为突出。娱乐和运输服
务 CPI 环比通胀率的上升是主要推动力， 两者均在10 月升到 0.8%
以上的较高水平。细看运输服务分项，油价涨幅的收窄推动机票
价格环比增速下滑（10 月：0.4%，9 月：0.4%）， 但汽车保险价格
浦银国际 
数据点评 
10月CPI再超预期，基本锁定12月美联储加息，升息幅度仍有不确定性 
金晓雯，PhD，CFA

### Page 2

10 月美国非农新增就业人数明显超预期， 有助于减轻美联储对劳动力
市场走弱的顾虑。10 月非农新增就业 16.5 万人， 远高于路透调查预期
的 6.5 万人，主要由专业和商业服务、医疗保健及运输仓储业拉动。8
月和 9 月新增就业分别上修至 18.6 万和 6.0 万人，合计上修 8.4 万人
（图表 2） 。在劳动参与率小幅上升的情况下（上升 0.1 个百分点到
61.9%） ， 失业率由9 月的 4.174%略微下降至 4.141%， 显示就业人数增
长快于劳动力人口 （图表 3） 。 不过， 因经济原因被迫兼职的人数增加，
广义失业率（U-6）由 7.6%升至 7.9%。与修订后的 9 月数据比较，私
营部门平均时薪环比增速由 0.3%加快至 0.4%，同比增速则由 3.2%微
降至 3.1%，工资趋势仍较温和。就业超预期减轻了美联储对劳动力市
场走弱的顾虑，但非农新增就业数据今年以来波动较大，我们认为并
不需要对单一数据过度解读。接下来，若财政刺激的边际提振减弱、
经济动能放缓， 叠加AI 应用压低部分岗位的招聘需求， 我们认为就业
增长或仍逐步降温。

10 月继续超预期的核心 CPI 数据再次凸显核心通胀粘性，高油价传导
效应开始显现。二手车价格已经回落、住房价格涨幅亦放缓，但核心
CPI 连续第三个月环比上涨 0.3%，说明基础价格压力尚未明显消退。
此外，此前仅主要反映在机票 CPI 的高油价传导效应 10 月开始向家
具、城际交通 CPI 等分项传导。不过， 各分项走势仍有分化， 单月数据
尚不足以确认全面再通胀。

接下来，油价回落有望缓和后续整体通胀，但核心通胀回落仍可能偏
慢。特朗普 11 月 9 日表示暂缓进一步对伊朗军事打击， 但仍维持海上
封锁，美伊尚未达成最终协议。布伦特原油期货 11 月 10 日盘中一度
跌破每桶 90 美元，较 10 月底高点明显回落。若局势继续缓和、油价
维持较低水平，汽油等能源分项对通胀的推升作用有望进一步减弱 。
然而，美联储 11 月 9 日发布的研究显示， 疫情后油价向核心通胀的传
导有所增强，前期高能源成本仍可能滞后影响商品与服务定价 ，这一
点在 10 月数据中已有所体现。 与此同时，AI 数据中心扩建可能加剧局
部电力和施工资源约束，尽管民主党赢得国会控制权以及部分关键州
州长竞选或对数据中心的建设形成一定阻力。不过，9 月实际可支配
收入环比下降 0.3%、实际消费近乎持平，家庭购买力承压或限制企业
向消费者转嫁成本的空间。

### Page 3

我们维持 12 月美联储加息 25 个基点的基准判断，但单次加息 50 个
基点的风险正在上升。10 月美联储会议表明通胀仍高、且为追加紧缩
保留依据，但沃什对政策已开始见效的评价增加了观望空间。 从基本
面来看，10 月就业超预期及此前月份数据上修缓解了美联储对劳动力
市场走弱的顾虑，核心 CPI 连续三个月上涨 0.3%则强化了追加紧缩的
理由。美联储理事沃勒 11 月 10 日在美国 CPI 数据公布前表示，若核
心 CPI 环比仍在 0.3%或更高，应考虑 12 月加息 50 个基点。本次核心
CPI 读数达到其提出的条件， 但这一表态仍属沃勒个人观点， 不能等同
于 FOMC 共识。 考虑到近期油价明显回落、 住房和核心商品涨幅放缓，
我们仍维持 12 月美联储再加息 25 个基点的基本判断，但由于超预期
的 10 月通胀和就业数据，12 月单次加息 50 个基点的风险也明显上
升，后续需关注其他官员是否跟进支持， 以及11 月非农就业数据、10
月 PCE 通胀数据发布和通胀预期变化。11 月 CPI 通胀数据发布时间将
晚于 12 月 8-9 日召开的 12 月议息会议。对于明年政策路径，需重点
关注 12 月经济预测及利率预测的调整，以及潜在的 5 个工作小组的报
告，后者可能也会影响美联储对利率路径的判断。目前来看，我们仍
倾向于认为美联储本轮是预防性加息，而非过热性加息。

在中期选举的影响问题上，我们认为国会控制权易手对美联储短期政
策的影响或有限。民主党控制两院减少了一项潜在的额外需求刺激，这
有助于降低进一步加息的尾部风险。此外， 当前更为制衡的美国政治环
境亦有可能放缓美国 AI 投资发展， 从而缓解从AI 投资扩张带来的价格
压力。不过，具体的货币政策走势仍依赖于通胀和就业之间的平衡。

另外，联邦法官、内阁及高级官员任命由参议院确认。在民主党控制
参议院的情况下，美联储理事的任命或受阻（地区联储行长不经参议
院确认） 。 未来任何美联储理事会的提名都有可能遭到冻结。 然而， 目
前七名理事席位均已满，沃什也已就任主席，短期内并不会自然形成
理事会席位长期空缺。 只有在2027 年后席位出现空缺， 民主党掌控下
的参议院才可通过延迟确认来限制特朗普继续调整理事会构成（参见：
民主党在美国中期选举中大获全胜，将如何影响美国经济和金融市
场？）。

风险提示： 通胀上行风险包括美伊局势反复推升油价， 前期能源成本
向核心商品和服务价格的传导强于预期， 以及AI 投资加剧电力、 原材
料和施工资源供需紧张，导致核心通胀粘性超预期，美联储加息幅度
或后续紧缩力度超过基准判断。下行风险包括美伊局势持续缓和、油
价回落快于预期，住房通胀降温加快，以及就业、消费或AI 投资走弱
超预期，促使通胀更快回落并降低进一步加息的必要性。

### Page 6

ベージュブック
（2026/7）
米国：AI関連を中心に製造業生産は堅調も、資材・設備の需給ひっ迫が他産業に波及
 米国の製造業生産は、AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子製品、一次金属、一般機械などが堅調
― 一方、非耐久財や家具など消費関連の生産は軟調に推移し、業種間のばらつきが拡大
 FRBは、データセンター建設に伴う鋼材・電気設備などの需要増加が、他の建設案件や生産活動に影響していると指摘
―AI投資の拡大が関連産業の生産を押し上げる一方、供給制約を通じて他産業の活動を圧迫する兆候も
― 当面はAI投資による成長押し上げ効果が期待される一方、供給制約や価格上昇を通じた負の影響にも注意が必要

5
 9月の全国コアCPIは前年比＋2.1％（前月：同＋1.7％）と加速。2025年12月以来の2％超に
― 原油高・円安等のコスト上昇もあり、家事用消耗品や教養娯楽用品等を中心に伸び率が拡大
― 一般サービス価格も伸び加速。シルバーウィークの並びによる宿泊料や外食費の上昇などが主因

日本：連合は2027年春闘で賃上げ要求を強化。高水準の賃上げ持続と物価への波及が焦点
6
 連合は、2027年春闘賃上げ要求の目安を「6％中心、5％以上」と発表
―5％以上の賃上げを最低目標として明確化。実質賃金1％上昇の定着をめざす方針
― 中小企業は大企業との格差是正に向け「6％以上」が目標。過去4年のインフレで実質賃金が低下した分の回復も要求
― また、新たに、2030年に2026年の賃金水準を15％程度上回る中期目標を設定。持続的な賃金上昇をめざす
 賃上げ余力は企業規模・業種で濃淡。中小企業等で高い「賃上げ5％の壁」
― なお、6％に近い賃上げが実現する場合、サービス価格への波及が強まり、基調的物価上昇率が2％を上回る可能性

欧州：2027年度予算審議が本格化。生活苦によるデモが拡大する中、財政赤字は高水準
7

市

回
顧
市場焦點
美國
科技股
• 美國企業獲利維持強勁 ，背後主要受經濟展望穩健及 AI資本支出熱潮支撐 。從獲利來看 ，市
場共識預期標普500企業EPS成長率2026年達28%，2027年及2028年則分別為20%及16%，
雖呈現逐步降溫 ，但仍維持雙位數增長 。標普500預期本益比已由約 23倍降至19倍，且並非
主要來自股價修正 ，而是企業獲利預期上調所致 ，在企業獲利維持成長 、AI投資持續挹注及
估值已較高點收斂的情況下，標普500仍具中長期配置價值。
美債
外匯對沖視角
• 在全球公債遭遇拋售潮與通膨陰影下 ，美債因利率期間結構與匯率優勢 ，今年以美元計價總
回報優於多數G7主權債，儘管海外私人機構投資人正在等待長端利率落底訊號以重返市場 ，
對美元資金或以美元計價為主的投資人而言 ，當前美債名目殖利率仍處於數十年高位 ，具備

相當良好的長期鎖利價值，但價格波動風險考量下，建議債券佈局仍以中短天期為主。
台股
• 8月景氣對策燈號續亮紅燈，綜合判斷分數41分，較前月持平，對應的燈號仍是代表景氣熱絡
的紅燈，連續第9個月亮出紅燈，追平史上最長「連紅」紀錄，顯示國內經濟維持成長。
• 根據歷史統計第四季台股上漲的機率接近八成 ，除了AI投資趨勢未減 ，年底集團作帳行情以
及美國11月期中選後不確定性下降有望支撐台股多頭情緒 。群益投顧樂觀看待台股第四季表
現，有望上看53,000點。此外，彭博綜合統計台灣加權指數12個月目標價達59,688點。

就業增幅明顯放緩，降低Fed短期再升息急迫性
• 非農明顯降溫，勞動市場尚未出現全面惡化：9月非農僅增2.9萬人，遠低於預期，且7、8月合計下修6萬人；失業率升
至4.2%，但勞參率同步升至 61.8%，家戶調查就業增加 40.6萬人，顯示失業率上升部分來自勞動供給增加 ，其中營建、
製造業仍分別增加1.1萬及0.9萬人，平均工時維持34.4小時，數據呈現招聘動能明顯放慢，但尚未出現全面性就業惡化。
• 薪資增速放緩，Fed短期政策壓力下降：9月平均時薪月增僅0.1%、年增3.0%，均低於前值；同時8月PCE年增3.4%、
核心PCE年增3.0%，顯示薪資通膨壓力有所緩和 ，但整體物價仍高於Fed 2%目標，在9月Fed升息1碼後，非農報告降
低Fed在10月連續升息的必要性，市場焦點轉向年底通膨與AI投資驅動的產業動能。

• 投資建議：短期關注美國中短天期投資級債券 ，就業降溫與薪資放緩有助降低短端利率上行壓力 ，但通膨仍高於目標 ，
因此不宜過度 預期長天期利率快速回落；股市則可續關注 AI基礎建設與資本支出相關產業 ，因營建及製造業就業仍具
韌性，顯示企業投資動能尚未明顯轉弱。
資料來源：U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics， 2026.10.3

消費動能回升、通膨低於預期，內需韌性與物價壓力並存
• PCE低於預期、通膨降溫仍待確認：美國8月PCE物價指數月增0.3%、年增3.4%，核心PCE月增0.2%、年增3.0%，均低於
市場預期；其中核心PCE較修訂後7月的3.3%下降，顯示物價壓力有所緩解，但仍高於Fed 2%目標，此次年度更新回溯修
訂2021年以來資料，亦使歷史基期重新調整，後續仍須觀察通膨下降是否能延續。
• 消費回升、所得增幅偏緩：8月個人所得月增0.2%，個人消費支出（PCE）月增0.9%、實質消費成長0.6%，顯示內需仍具
韌性；惟消費增幅明顯高於所得 ，個人儲蓄率由7月4.6%降至4.1%，為2022年11月以來低點，反映家庭可支應消費的財
務緩衝持續縮小，後續消費動能仍須觀察。
• 後市觀察：PCE低於預期後，市場對10月再升息的預期明顯降溫，但長端美債殖利率仍受通膨、財政供給與期限溢價牽動

而維持高檔；建議可聚焦獲利成長與現金流穩健的優質大型成長股與短天期美債以掌握收益 ，同時留意儲蓄率下滑與 9月
消費者信心低迷可能抑制後續消費動能，動態調整部位並嚴控風險。
資料來源：U.S. Bureau of Economic Analysis， 2026.10.1
PCE與核心PCE年增率(%)走勢 八月消費支出各類別分項比較

能源與AI推升長端利率，融資成本壓力延續
• 長端殖利率上行 、期限溢價成重要推力 ：近期
美債呈現短端與長端走勢分化 ，雖市場對 10月
Fed再升息預期因 8月PCE低於預期而明顯降溫 ，
10年期及30年期的美債殖利率仍偏高位 ，反映
通膨風險 、財政融資需求與期限溢價上升 ，長
天期無風險利率維持高檔 ，並推升房貸及企業
融資成本。
• 能源與AI投資推升通膨及資金需求 ：油價維持
高檔增加通膨黏著風險 ，AI基礎建設投資則帶
動企業資本支出與融資需求 ，兩者共同提高長
期資金需求；在財政赤字與美債供給仍大的環
境下，長端殖利率可能維持較高水準 ，進而提
高股票估值折現率及高槓桿企業的融資壓力。
• 後市觀察： 短期持續觀察油價 、10年及30年期
美債殖利率與期限溢價變化；債券配置仍建議

降低長天期債券部位以降低利率風險 ，優先布
局短天期高品質債券獲取穩健息收 ，股票則聚
焦具穩健現金流 、低財務槓桿與定價能力的企
業，以降低高利率環境下的估值與融資風險。
資料來源：Bloomberg，Federal Reserve Bank of New Tork ， 2026.10.1
長天期美債殖利率曲線與期限溢價變動趨勢
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.1

AI需求大增，美光財測超過市場預期
• AI記憶體需求強勁，美光財測顯示景氣延續：美光科技9/30盤後公布財報，2026財年的第四季營收達542.3億美元，年增

379%，調整後EPS 33.42美元，均優於市場預期；公司並預估2027會計年度第一季營收615億美元、調整後EPS 38.15美元，
分別高於市場預期的567.7億美元及36.02美元。強勁財測反映AI資料中心持續推升HBM、伺服器DRAM及高速SSD需求。
隨Amazon、Alphabet等大型科技企業持續擴大AI基礎建設投資，記憶體需求仍具成長動能。
• 長約與產品升級，支撐中長期業績：美光目前已簽署36份多年期供應協議，涵蓋約三分之一營收並延伸至2030年，有助降
低記憶體產業週期波動；同時持續布局HBM4、HBM4E及高容量DDR5等產品。公司2026會計年度自由現金流達590億美元，
並預期2027、2028會計年度供需將進一步緊俏。整體而言，AI需求已由單一HBM擴展至伺服器記憶體與儲存產品，美光財
測亦顯示景氣延續性仍強，後續市場焦點將轉向擴產速度、資本支出及AI需求成長能否維持。
資料來源：美光，彭博，鉅亨網，2026.10.1

• AI驅動獲利成長，企業基本面仍具支撐：美國企業獲利維持強勁，背後主要受經濟展望穩健及AI資本支出熱潮支撐。從獲利
來看，市場共識預期標普500企業EPS成長率2026年達28%，2027年及2028年則分別為20%及16%，雖呈現逐步降溫，但
仍維持雙位數增長。其中，AI基礎建設與大型雲端業者仍是重要驅動來源，2026年對EPS成長的貢獻合計約19%，2027、
2028年仍分別貢獻14%及11%。
• 獲利上修降低估值壓力：標普500預期本益比已由約23倍降至19倍，且並非主要來自股價修正，而是企業獲利預期上調所致。
雖然未來EPS成長率若由高檔回落，市場仍可能面臨評價修正壓力，但在企業獲利維持成長、AI投資持續挹注及估值已較高
點收斂的情況下，標普500基本面仍具支撐。因此，短期應留意獲利增速放緩與市場持倉偏高所帶來的波動，但從獲利成長

與估值兩大面向觀察，標普500仍具中長期配置價值。

BCI:企業利潤前瞻指數 BCI:企業銷售前瞻指數
2026年製造業PMI 2024年 2025年 2023年
生產回升與價格上漲同步推動中國PMI改善
• 生產回升與價格上漲同步推動 中國PMI改善： 9月製造業PMI升至50.1%，較前月回升0.3個百分點，重返榮枯線以上，顯示
製造業景氣有所改善 ，主要受生產活動回暖及價格上漲帶動 ，中小企業景氣同步改善 ，但仍低於榮枯線，大型企業表現相對
較佳，高技術製造、裝備製造維持擴張 ，消費品PMI升至50.7%，重回景氣區間 ，另一方面，原材料及能源價格上漲推升企
業成本，上下游獲利差距持續擴大 。 非製造業PMI則由49.0%升至50.2%，服務業受節慶效應帶動回溫 ，建築業則受財政資

金投入，及基建建設推進而改善，不過需求復甦仍偏溫和，企業成本壓力尚未明顯減輕。
• 結論：第四季隨著基建投資、財政支出及房市支持政策逐步落地，經濟可望獲得支撐，但10月受長假因素影響，PMI可能短
期承壓。

台灣景氣燈號連9紅 台股Q4有望突破5萬點大關
• 8月景氣對策燈號續亮紅燈： 綜合判斷分數41分，較前月持平，對應的燈號仍是代表景氣熱絡的紅燈 ，連續第9個月亮出紅
燈，追平史上最長「連紅」紀錄，顯示國內經濟維持成長。國發會表示，AI需求令台廠積極擴廠，加上政府推動AI新十大建
設，民間投資有望維持動能。此外，受惠財富效應、企業調薪及就業市場穩定，民間消費有望持續成長。
• 法人看好台股年底前站上5萬點：根據歷史統計第四季台股上漲的機率接近八成 ，除了AI投資趨勢未減，年底集團作帳行情
以及美國11月期中選後不確定性下降有望支撐台股多頭情緒 。群益投顧樂觀看待台股第四季表現 ，有望上看53,000點。此
外，彭博綜合統計台灣加權指數12個月目標價達59,688點。
• 觀點：近期台股站上48,000點整數關卡，總市值達5.3兆美元，讓台股耀升成為全球第四大市值股市 ，顯示台股在AI浪潮下

的得天獨厚。年底前仍須留意中東衝突進展，若能源通膨壓力持續，聯準會升息預期恐為股市帶來波動，但考慮AI科技持續
發酵，建議投資人續抱台股，並以定期不定額的策略紀律加碼，目標追求中長期完整的AI趨勢回報。
資料來源：國發會，2026.09.29。

外匯對沖視角—美債對沖後殖利率屬全球最低之一
• 美國國債殖利率真的高嗎？在當前全球總體經濟環境下，美國國債殖利率雖維持在數十年歷史高點附近，但若單憑名目殖利
率來判斷美債的吸引力，容易忽視匯率對沖與各國財政風險帶來的溢價差異。
• 美元視角下的殖利率比較： 若將其他已開發市場的主權債務 (如德債、日債、英債)完全對沖回美元，美債在殖利率曲線上多
數期限的實際殖利率，其實與德債並列全球最低水準。相反地，日本國債與英國國債經匯率對沖回美元後的收益率反而更高。
• 跨貨幣資產交換 (ASW)的真實回報： 以10年期主權債的對沖跨貨幣資產交換計算：美國 10年期國債有效殖利率約 4.85%，
日本10年期國債(對沖至美元)有效殖利率約5.06%，德國10年期國債(對沖至美元)有效殖利率約4.51%，這表明美國政府發

債的真實對沖融資成本其實已相對精實，且符合國際市場對各國財政基本面的合理定價。
對沖至美元後的10年期主權債殖利率
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.29

財政基本面與信用風險—殖利率溢價反映國家債務比重
• 從歐元計價或信用風險的角度來比較 ，美債的定價邏輯非常符合總體經濟基本面： 儘管債務以歐元計價時 ，10年期美債殖
利率比同期限德國國債高出約30個基點，這反映出德國的債務佔GDP比重僅約65%，而美國則高達127%，因此美債承擔了
適度的財政溢價。儘管義大利的債務佔GDP比重高達140%且信用前景較遜色，但在統一貨幣基準下，義大利國債殖利率仍
較美國國債高出60個基點，這顯示市場並未過度壓低美債信用，美債依然享有優質避險資產的溢價優勢。
• 海外私人投資者的資金流向：近一年海外私人機構在美債加碼速度放緩，乃通膨環境下全球其他資產選擇增加，及過去升息
波段導致帳面按市值計價虧損 。然而歐洲投資人(持有一半以上的海外美債)目前仍持有龐大部位，並在等待適當的加碼時機。

• 小結與投資建議：在經過外匯對沖後，美債收益率在發達國家主權債中處於相對低位，發行成本合理且具競爭力。美債殖利
率高於德債低於義債 ，精確地反映美國政府債務負擔率 (127%)與總體經濟穩健度 。在全球公債遭遇拋售潮與通膨陰影下 ，
美債因利率期間結構與匯率優勢 ，今年以美元計價總回報優於多數 G7主權債，儘管海外私人機構投資人正在等待長端利率
落底訊號以重返市場，對美元資金或以美元計價為主的投資人而言，當前美債名目殖利率仍處於數十年高位，具備相當良好
的長期鎖利價值，但價格波動風險考量下，建議債券佈局仍以中短天期為主。
歐元計價美債殖利率高於德但低於義

亞洲晶⽚債券跑輸指數，評級保持穩健
• 亞洲晶⽚債券跑輸指數，評級保持穩健：儘管AI熱潮推動收⼊創下紀錄 ，但SK海力⼠和台積電的美元債 ，今年以來的表現仍
略遜於彭博亞洲美元信用債總回報指數 ，主因為大規模資本支出擴張 ，以及潛在的投⼊資本回報率下降令 其利差承壓，美國
雲服務巨頭創紀錄的 AI支出計畫加劇了壓力 ，儘管如此 SK 海力⼠和台積電仍保持較強的高評級信用實力 ，台積電計畫投
⼊600-640億美元資本支出 ，SK海力⼠的資本支出則處於 40兆韓圜區間。兩家公司穩健的資產負債表 、合計共1,260億美元
的淨現金、強勁的經營性現金流，以及在先進製程，和HBM晶⽚領域的市場領先地位，為上述支出提供支撐。
• 投資建議：現階段台積電與SK海力⼠美元債最大的問題不是信用風險 ，而是估值與利差壓力 ，短期可能因AI資本支出過大，

及市場情緒波動而表現落後指數 ，但以兩家公司強大的現金流 、淨現金部位及產業領導地位來看 ，信用品質仍屬亞洲投資級
債中的優質標的，建議利差擴大時分批布局 。

顧 • 企業獲利成長將支撐股價上行：標普500指數的長期走勢與企業每股盈餘（EPS）高度連動。高盛預估，標普500企業EPS將
於2026年達375美元，並在2027年進一步升至415美元，對應年增率約36%與11%。在利率維持高檔、金融條件偏緊的環境
下，企業獲利仍持續處於成長軌道，將為股市提供穩健的中期上行動能。
• 本益比回落提供潛在布局契機：根據FactSet數據，標普500企業第三季整體獲利預計成長30.6%。其中，資訊科技類股獲利
成長預估高達66.5%，領先能源以外的其他產業；同時，該類股預估本益比已由今年高點回落，在獲利上修與估值修正同步
發生的背景下，浮現具吸引力的中期布局機會。
• 小結：短期市場仍可能受地緣政治與利率預期擾動，但中期趨勢仍由企業獲利擴張所主導。建議投資人採取分批布局科技股

策略，聚焦具備AI受益結構、資本支出能見度高及現金流穩健的龍頭企業。

1. 本資料內容僅供內部同仁及客戶參考，非作為任何金融商品或服務之購買或銷售之推介，不得視為買賣有價證券或其他金融商品之要約或要約之引誘。
2. 本資料提及之經濟走勢預測不必然代表基金之績效，基金投資風險請詳閱基金公開說明書。
3. 基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益 ，投資人申購
前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。

公司拜訪快報

聯強(2347)：資金成本上揚壓抑短期營收動能，但長期營益率改善趨勢
不變 
建議：維持買進

一、聯強為亞太地區最大 3C 通
路商，營收成長以半導體業務最
佳  
 聯強為 3C 電子產品之銷售、配送、維修、量身訂做即時生產之專業通路商，為
亞太地區 3C 通路商龍頭，2026 年前三季產品比重分別為商用加值佔 31%、資
訊消費佔 17%、 半 導 體 佔 48%、 手 機 產 品 佔4% ，成長主要來商用加值
(+112%YoY)和半導體業務(+162%YoY)。聯強海外市場包含中國、香港、紐澳、
泰國、印尼、印度及越南等地，其中以中國、台灣和印尼等區域較佳，營收分別
為 YoY+109%、YoY+78%和 YoY+18%。聯強競爭優勢為透過其四合一經營模
式，搭配其綿密的通路發揮規模經濟，並提供高附加價值的後勤運籌 (庫存管理
與快速維為修服務等)，於中國主要競爭對手為神州數碼、天音通信控股。

二、半導體業務成長性佳，3Q26 
EPS 2.93 元創新高並優於預期 
 3Q26 營收 2366 億元(+18%QoQ；+136%YoY)優於預估值 14%，其中商用加
值和半導體營收分別為+116%YOY 和+273%YoY，因秉棄部分利潤不佳專案，
毛利率 3.49%優於前季之 3.4%，營業利益 61.41 億元(+28%%QoQ)優於預估
值 24%，因營運資金需求以及籌資成本上升致使利息費用提高至 9.34 億元
(+106%QoQ)，稅後純益 48.83 億元(+25%QoQ；+90%YoY)，EPS 2.93 元優
於預期之 2.5 元並創新高。

三、資金成本上揚導致業務拓展
較為謹慎，估計 4Q26 EPS 2.34
元 
 由於資金成本上揚致使公司放棄部分利潤不佳半導體業務 ， 十月營收月減34%，
估計 4Q26 營收 2046 億元(-14%QoQ；YoY+62%)，因產品組合變化轉佳，毛
利率提升至 3.58%，稅後純益 38.99 億元(-20%QoQ；+41%YoY)，EPS 2.34
元。

四、AI 長線需求有助商用加值和
半導體業務成長性 
 集邦 預估 2026 和 2027 年全 球九 大 CSP 資 本支 出分 別為 0.95 兆 美元
(+106%YoY)和 1.53 兆美元(+60%YoY)，且 AI 需求從雲端擴展到企業端，其中
以商用加值和半導體業務受惠幅度最大。 
在商用加值業務方面，在台灣和中國資料中心產品出貨挹注下， 3Q26 營收
+116%YoY。展望 2027 年，中國十十五規劃和國產化政策推動政府和國企加速
採用本土品牌 ， 推升聯強代理華為 、 浪潮等品牌業務成長性 ， 台灣則是接獲數個
半導體大廠 AI 基礎建設專案 ， 加計中小企業AI 建置需求逐步顯現 ， 商用業務將
為營運成長主要動能之一。 
在半導體業務方面，缺貨和漲價效致使記憶體、SSD 和硬碟業務為半導體業務
中 成 長 性 最 佳 者 ， 且AI 推 論 亦 增 加 CPU 需求， 3Q26 半 導 體 業 務 營 收

YoY+273%，半導體業務為營運成長另一重點業務，但因資金成本上揚，公司將
摒棄部分利潤未達目標專案，此將影響短期營收動能。 
受惠於出貨 ASP 增加，以及原廠擴大促銷力道挽救銷售量，3Q26 資訊家用和
手機業務分別+21%YoY 和+33%YoY。 記憶體大漲致使原廠逐步反應成本於PC
和手機新機售價，並將導致消費者延長換機週期，2027 年此兩塊業務成長性仍
相對保守看待。

五、資金成本上揚將致使接案更
為謹慎，但長期營益率好轉方向
不變 
 聯強今年接獲數個大型 AI 基礎建設專案 ， 致使營收動能以及營運資金需求大增 ，
3Q26 遇到資金成本上揚，致使利息費用較前季倍增，公司將透過聯貸、可轉債
以及現增降低利息負擔，預期三項籌資工具皆完成後可望降低利息費用 20-
30%。展望 2027 年，儘管營收成長性不如 2026 年亮眼，但接單策略轉趨謹慎
將致使毛利率下滑壓力降低 ， 且預期營業費用增幅有限下 ， 營益率持續好轉方向
不變。

六、維持買進理由  1)預估 2026 和 2027 年 EPS 分別為 9.38 元和 11.07 元。 
2)儘管資金成本上揚壓抑短期營收動能 ， 但長期營益率改善趨勢不變 ， 維持買進
評等，TP 100 元 (2027 PE x 9)。

King Slide Works Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
October Sales +2% MoM/+191%
YoY
In this report, we focus on King Slide's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price movement.
Details::
• October sales were NT$4,562mn (+2% MoM/+191% YoY)
Our view:
• We think the monthly sales run rate in the near term should stay roughly at
the Sep-26 level and might start to tick up from late 4Q26/early 1Q27 along
with the new server platform ramp.

• Near-term softness should be well known and dilution from the recently
announced CB issuance is manageable (up to 1.4% dilution based on the
latest price), while the growth outlook still looks promising for 2027 amid
the increasing penetration of rail kits into more hardware devices in AI
clusters.
• We maintain our view that King Slide's competitiveness in the comprehensive
patent portfolio and strong execution are entrenched and accentuated amid

fast generation migration in AI applications.
• Stay OW.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
King Slide Works Co. Ltd. (2059.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it better captures King Slide's earnings growth potential. We believe our target mul-
tiple is justified by an operating profit CAGR of 77% seen for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.4
vs. the average of 1.2 over 2010-24, when the stock was trading at 17-18x P/E with an operating

profit CAGR of 14%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected AI capex
n Stronger server demand
n Faster market share gains
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Weaker server demand
n Sluggish kitchen furniture demand
n Competition leading to slower share gains.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Investment case: We initiate coverage of Hon Precision, the global leader 
in test handlers and active thermal control (ATC) systems, with a Buy (1) 
rating. According to management, the company commands >70% market 
share in AI/HPC final test (FT) and c.50% in system-level test (SLT) by 
revenue, distinctly positioning it as one of the key global beneficiaries of the 
rising silicon complexity.

Multi-layer content stacking supports ASP and margin growth. As test 
durations continue to lengthen and thermal requirements rise, customers 
are required to deploy greater parallel testing capacity and higher-
specification handlers, creating a structural demand tailwind for both FT 
and SLT handler. We forecast rising AI chip power consumption and 
package complexity over our forecast horizon to drive adoption of higher-
value ATC systems, mega-tray handlers (adding 25-30% to ASP relative to 
standard dual-temp ATC systems), and AOI integration (adding a further 
10-15% to system ASP), supporting a multi-year increase in content per 
tool and expanding its gross margin from 56.8% to 59.2% over 2026-28E.

Recurring consumables and CPO testing expand long-term growth 
visibility. Cold plates generate high-margin, annuity-like revenue with 
every new chip platform which should benefit from shortening AI processor 
refresh cycles. Beyond consumables, Hon Precision is extending its testing 
platform into co-packaged optics (CPO), where optical-electrical co-testing 
could unlock a new addressable market beyond traditional GPU and ASIC 
testing. While near-term contribution remains limited, we view CPO as a 
strategic growth option that enhances the company’s long-term earnings 
visibility and valuation premium over our forecast horizon.

Catalysts: 1) capacity expansion ramp in 2027; 2) mega-tray penetration 
and higher-power ATC adoption; 3) initial CPO test solution qualification.

Valuation: We initiate with a Buy (1) rating and 12M TP of TWD6,365, 
based on a target PER of 30x applied on our 2027E EPS. The stock 
currently trades at c.19x forward PER, despite our projected 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 55%, implying a PEG of only 0.35x. We believe its current 
valuation heavily discounts macro noise with share price pulling back by 
c.53% from its historical high and derating from a cycle-peak multiple of 45-
50x 1-year-forward PER. Backed by solid order visibility through late-2027, 
we highlight Hon Precision’s risk-reward profile as decisively asymmetric.

Risks: 1) Slower-than-expected AI/HPC investment; 2) delayed ramp-up of 
new products; and 3) potential loss of market share.

Growth outlook

Hon Precision: revenue and gross margin outlook 
We expect Hon Precision’s growth momentum over 2026-
28E to be driven by increasing testing content per chip, 
supported by rising power consumption in AI/HPC chips, 
larger package sizes, and growing system complexity. We 
expect the transition towards high-end ATC handlers, 
mega-tray solutions, and integrated AOI functions to drive 
meaningful ASP expansion over our forecast horizon, with 
recurring cold plate demand providing an additional gross-
margin growth driver.

Source: Company, Daiwa research

Valuation   Hon Precision: 1-year-forward PER bands 
Our 12-month TP of TWD6,365 is based on a target PER 
of 30x applied to our 2027E EPS forecast. Following the 
strong share-price rally in 1H26, investor focus shifted 
away from Hon Precision due to limited near-term catalysts 
ahead of the 2H27 ramp-up of mega-tray handlers, CPO 
testing solutions and new capacity. We believe the recent 
53% pullback from its historical high on 29 May 2026 and 
derating from 45-50x 1-year-forward PER to c.19x more 
than prices in the macro uncertainties. At the current levels, 
the stock offers an attractive risk-reward profile, trading at 
a PEG of just 0.35x based on our 2026-28E EPS CAGR of 
55%.

Source: Bloomberg

Earnings revisions

Hon Precision: Bloomberg-consensus earnings revisions 
The Bloomberg consensus raised its 2026E and 2027E 
EPS estimates by 68% and 122%, respectively, over the 
past year, driven by stronger-than-expected AI-related 
demand and improving visibility on the company’s long-
term growth trajectory. We believe the upward revisions 
reflect rising confidence in volume and ASP expansion 
across the company’s key testing solutions, supported by 
accelerating AI infrastructure investments and expanding 
customer adoption.

Company profile 
Founded in 1999, Hon Precision is the global leader in semiconductor test handlers and active thermal 
control (ATC) systems. The company dominates the global AI/HPC final test handler market, supported 
by its advanced thermal management technologies. With over 70% market share in AI/HPC test 
handling, Hon Precision benefits from growing demand for AI, HPC and custom ASIC applications. Its 
solutions are widely adopted by leading OSATs and foundries worldwide, making it a key enabler of 
advanced semiconductor testing.

### Page 6

6

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Growth Catalysts 
Multi-factor dollar content stacking 
We believe the primary growth catalyst for Hon Precision over our forecast horizon stems 
from multi-layered dollar content stacking, where the rise in machine unit value should 
outpace volume growth. Next-gen AI accelerators have historically exhibited more than 50-
70% increase in thermal design power (TDP) per generation, with platforms like Nvidia 
Rubin and Rubin Ultra surging to 2.3kW and 3.6kW vs. Blackwell’s 1.2kW to 1.4kW. 
Managing these extreme thermal loads makes high-end active thermal control (ATC) a 
necessity, driving a structural step-up in ASPs from standard handlers at approximately 
TWD6m to dual-temperature ATC systems at TWD12-15m, and tri-temperature ATC 
systems at TWD15-18m.

Concurrently, as the package size of next-generation ASICs/GPUs increases from the 
current 120mm tray specification to around 150mm, Hon Precision is introducing mega-
tray large-package solutions in October 2026, which command a 25-30% ASP premium 
over mainstream GPU handlers. As the majority of next-generation ASIC/GPU is set to 
exceed the boundary of 120mm, we estimate shipment penetration for mega-tray solutions 
will likely reach 40% in 2027E. Integrated functions of Automated Optical Inspection (AOI) 
will contribute an additional 10-15% ASP uplift to unit value of handler. Furthermore, this 
content expansion extends into the flagship mobile AP market as smartphone processors 
transition to multi-die packaging. For example, Apple’s A20 Pro processor has already 
adopted wafer-level multi-chip module (WMCM) packaging in 2026, requiring high-end ATC 
systems capable of managing up to 3,000W thermal dissipation to resolve device self-
heating.

Hon Precision: ASP multipliers

Source: Daiwa research

High-margin cold plate consumables  
In addition to capital equipment set sales, Hon Precision’s cold plate consumables 
business serves as a crucial recurring revenue engine and a primary driver of gross margin 
expansion. Cold plates carry the highest gross margin across all company product lines. 
Driven by the compression of AI processor lifecycles to 1-1.5 years (down from 2-3 years 
historically), each test handler requires 5-10 distinct, custom-engineered cold plates 
tailored to specific die footprints, socket layouts, and thermal hot-spot geometries. To 
conduct parallel test and maintain the throughput of chip testing, test handler for system 
level test (SLT) requires even more cold plates, which can go up to 24 plates. As a result, 
cold plate tooling generates high-frequency annuity-like sales that recur with every new 
chip platform iteration, independent of major equipment replacements.

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Hon Precision: cold plate revenue trend

Source: Company, Daiwa research

Testing duration and volume multiples 
We believe the rapid rise in AI chip complexity is driving structural growth in semiconductor 
testing intensity, positioning Hon Precision as a key global beneficiary through its 
leadership in high-parallelism test handlers. Driven by the mainstream adoption of 
heterogeneous integration (such as TSMC’s CoWoS and Intel’s EMIB), verifying complex 
AI silicon has extended test durations by 2x to 10x vs. standard consumer chips (5 to 10 
seconds). Final test durations for AI GPUs have expanded from hundreds of seconds to 
over 10 minutes per chip. To maintain daily manufacturing throughput despite this 10x 
spike, chipmakers must scale up massive parallel testing architectures and procure 
significantly more handlers, cementing Hon Precision as the primary test handler partner 
for tier-one US AI GPU and CPU players.

Concurrently, escalating chip complexity, TDP, and die costs make field return (RMA) 
penalties far exceed testing expenses, forcing cloud service providers (CSPs) and 
automotive OEMs to mandate system-level testing (SLT). Because SLT executes over 
extended durations ranging from minutes to hours per chip, high-volume production 
mandates automated parallel SLT handlers equipped with high-capacity ATC. Holding 
c.50% market share in AI/HPC SLT handlers alongside Chroma, Hon Precision is 
expanding beyond GPUs into custom CSP ASICs (eg, Google TPU and AWS) and server 
CPUs. As SLT adoption broadens across non-GPU devices, Hon Precision leverages this 
multi-year runway to transition into a full-spectrum semiconductor testing platform.

Global: growing chip complexity – testing time by AI/HPC chip

Source: Daiwa research

CPO unlocks new TAM beyond GPUs 
Beyond conventional electrical testing, Co-Packaged Optics (CPO) represents a multi-year 
paradigm shift towards a multimodal optical-electrical testing platform. Hon Precision 
currently monetises initial CPO deployments by supplying Final Test (FT) handlers for pure 
electrical testing (E/E test) of switch ASICs and Optical Engines (OEs) on production 
platforms such as Nvidia’s Spectrum-X and Quantum-X.

### Page 8

8

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026

However, the strategic growth catalyst is anchored in next-generation Insertion 4 (package-
level optical and electrical co-testing, O/E co-test). Because an undetected defect in a 
single optical component would force manufacturers to scrap an entire high-value CPO 
module, early and rigorous package-level validation is imperative for yield protection. 
Collaborating directly with ATE leader Teradyne and optical instrument specialists (such as 
Keysight), Hon Precision is developing a specialised ATC handler capable of simultaneous 
optical and electrical validation, including Bit Error Rate (BER), optical loopback, crosstalk, 
and high-power thermal management testing for integrated ASIC and Optical Engine 
modules in a single touchdown.

Engaged with Nvidia CPO switch programmes and actively expanding to additional ASIC 
clients, Hon Precision targets engineering verification in late-2026 and initial shipments in 
2027, ahead of 2028 volume production. Although we expect revenue contribution will 
likely be relatively low (<5% in 2027-2028), it drives valuation and market expectation to 
serve larger total addressable market (TAM).

Market Analysis 
Global testing equipment market 
According to TechInsights, global testing equipment capex is projected to expand 
significantly from USD8.3bn in 2024 to USD26.1bn by 2027E, representing a 46.4% 
CAGR. Notably, while SoC test systems front-loaded strong growth in 2024-25, before 
tapering to 9.6% YoY in 2027E on TechInsights estimates, package handling equipment is 
forecasted to exhibit strong structural resilience and longevity. Based on TechInsights, 
market capex for package handling equipment will likely more than double from USD941m 
in 2024 to USD2.6bn by 2027E (40.9% CAGR), re-accelerating to 46% YoY growth in 
2027E from 34% YoY in 2025. This cadence reflects the phased deployment of test 
infrastructure – initial tester investments are followed by high-volume back-end handling 
installations as advanced packaging (eg, CoWoS, Chiplets) scales into mass production.

Hon Precision’s overall share in the global test handler TAM rose from 35% in 2024 to 57%

market share, exceeding 70% in both sales value and shipment volume, per management. 
In the rapidly expanding AI and HPC SLT handler segment, Hon Precision holds c.50% 
market share in terms of revenue, per management, alongside its primary peer, Chroma, 
establishing a duopoly in global high-end SLT testing.

### Page 10

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026

While global incumbents rely on brand prestige, standard architectures, and bundled 
offerings, Hon Precision excels in leading-edge AI/HPC testing through its ultra-high-power 
Active Thermal Control (ATC) and rapid engineering turnaround. As multi-kilowatt dynamic 
loads (1.5kW–3kW+) and extreme warpage on >120mm packages push standard handlers 
beyond their limits, Hon Precision’s hyper-customised ATC fluidics, high-force micro-
mechanics, and modular designs deliver superior millisecond thermal clamping and agile 
package adaptation. Furthermore, while giants like Advantest dominate the SoC ATE tester 
market (>55% share), their in-house handlers focus primarily on memory and standard 
logic. Consequently, tier-one chipmakers and OSATs standardly decouple tester and 
handler sourcing, thus pairing Advantest or Teradyne testers with Hon Precision’s high-
power ATC handlers. Located directly within Taiwan’s advanced packaging cluster, Hon 
Precision provides real-time, on-site co-validation and custom cold-plate engineering that 
preserve formidable entry barriers against bundled solutions.

Meanwhile, although Chinese competitors have expanded aggressively in domestic mature 
nodes and conventional Pick-and-Place handlers, Hon Precision commands a decisive 
generational lead in high-complexity advanced packaging applications. Chinese vendors 
remain largely confined to lower-wattage consumer, automotive, and industrial IC testing, 
lacking the proven patents, structural rigidity, and micro-level force balancing required to 
safely apply hundreds of kilograms of contact force onto fragile, multi-chip CoWoS 
packages without cracking dies or warping substrates. Moreover, because enterprise AI 
chips cost tens of thousands of dollars each, tier-1 global fabless leaders and North 
American cloud service providers (CSPs) maintain virtually zero tolerance for testing 
failure, establishing an entrenched validation moat for Hon Precision. Free from 
geopolitical export constraints, Hon Precision can seamlessly deploy across global OSAT 
networks in Taiwan, North America, and Southeast Asia.

Balance sheet and cash flow 
Driven by surging AI/HPC testing demand, Hon Precision is actively expanding its capacity 
across the new Desheng facility, localised production at the Changshu site, and 
debottlenecking of headquarter efficiency. The company targets 40-50% capacity growth in 
2026 and another 50% increase in 2027. The expansion of manufacturing space and 
production facilities requires an increase in capital expenditure, which we project Hon 
Precision will ramp up to approximately TWD2.8bn in 2026E, TWD3.8bn in 2027E, and 
TWD4.6bn in 2028E. The company’s operating cash inflows are more than sufficient to 
fully fund factory expansions alongside a c.70% dividend payout ratio.

Hon Precision continues to maintain an un-levered balance sheet with zero financial bank 
debt. Cash and cash equivalents exceeded TWD53bn in 2025; we project this figure to 
surpass TWD75bn by 2028E. Its robust balance sheet underpins peer-leading shareholder 
returns, with ROE to expand from 34.5% in 2025 to 59.6% by 2028E. This self-funding 
cash engine supports management’s targeted c.70% dividend payout ratio.

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Valuation and recommendation 
Initiating coverage with a Buy (1) rating 
We initiate coverage of Hon Precision with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD6,365, 
based on a target PER of 30x applied to our 2027E EPS forecast. When the stock rallied 
above TWD8,000 in 1H26, its forward PER multiple stretched to a cycle peak of 45-50x. 
However, we expect key growth catalysts, including a 50% aggregate capacity expansion, 
alongside higher-ASP shipments of large-form-factor mega tray handlers and co-packaged 
optics (CPO) test solutions, to drive its top line, primarily in 2H27. With existing production 
lines operating at full utilisation, investors are concerned over a potential temporary 
deceleration in growth momentum through 2H26-1H27, ahead of the next potential major 
capacity inflection in 2H27.

However, as the calendar rolls into 2027, we believe market attention will swiftly refocus on 
the company’s multi-year structural thesis. Easing capacity bottlenecks, combined with 
robust ASIC-driven adoption of mega tray architectures and ramping CPO test platforms, 
should spark a powerful volume-and-ASP expansion cycle. The stock is currently trading at 
a 19x 1-year-forward PER vs. its earnings CAGR of 55% over 2026-28E, translating into a 
0.35x PEG ratio. We believe the valuation now heavily discounts the near-term pause in 
revenue and macro noises, presenting a compelling risk-reward profile for long-term 
investors.

Risks to our call 
1. Slower-than-expected AI/HPC investment: A slowdown in AI infrastructure spending 
or delays in GPU, ASIC and server CPU ramps could reduce demand for high-end 
FT/SLT handlers and thermal-control solutions, weakening order growth and revenue 
visibility. 
2. Slower-than-expected new product adoption: Customer qualification delays or 
slower adoption of mega-tray handlers, AOI inspection systems and CPO FT solutions 
could delay product ramps, resulting in revenue growth below our expectations. 
3. Potential share loss: Competitors’ advances in thermal control, customer qualification 
of alternative suppliers or delays in meeting new testing requirements could weaken 
Hon Precision’s position on next-generation platforms, affecting market share and 
pricing power.

### Page 15

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Appendix 
Company introduction 
Hon Precision was founded in Taichung in 2015, building on the technology and industry 
experience of Hon Technology, which was established in 1999. The company develops 
semiconductor test handlers and thermal-control solutions for AI GPUs, AI ASICs, server 
CPUs, automotive chips and consumer electronics, supporting customers from new-
product introduction (NPI) through volume production.

Hon Precision has two main product categories:

• Semiconductor test handlers: Final-test (FT) and system-level-test (SLT) handlers 
automate chip loading, positioning and sorting. These platforms can incorporate active 
thermal control (ATC) systems to maintain chips at specified temperatures during 
testing. 
• Testing fixtures and modules: Mainly customised cold plates and socket layout kits 
(SLKs), which provide thermal management and mechanical interfaces for different chip 
packages. Customers may require replacement cold plates and kits as package designs 
change, even when retaining existing handlers.

Hon Precision’s order mix is significantly weighted towards AI/HPC/ASIC applications, 
which accounted for 78% of orders in 1Q-3Q26, up from 72% in 2025. These applications 
include AI computing, servers and networking. Automotive represented 9% of orders, 
followed by mobile AP and communications at 7%, consumer electronics at 4%, and 
memory and MEMS at 2%.

As for capacity, Hon Precision’s manufacturing footprint is primarily based in Taiwan, with 
Taichung serving as the group’s main R&D, engineering, quality management, and 
equipment production hub. In addition, the company operates a Suzhou facility focused on 
manufacturing cold plates and related kits, while Taichung Plant 4 is expected to provide 
incremental production capacity to support future growth.

Shareholder structure 
Hon Precision’s major shareholders include several investment and development 
companies. As of 17 April 2026, Hung Min Development was the largest shareholder with a 
17.37% stake, followed by Hongji Investment at 12.08% and Hong Yu Development at 
7.80%. Chairman Wen-Ta Hsieh also directly held 1.77%. International institutional 
shareholders included Norges Bank and GIC, with disclosed stakes of 1.30% and 0.97%, 
respectively. The company has a free float of 48%, allowing broad public-market 
participation and supporting trading liquidity.

### Page 17

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Appendix 
What is handler? 
A semiconductor test handler automates the movement, positioning and temperature 
conditioning of packaged chips during testing. It places each device into a test socket, 
applies the required contact force and sorts the device according to the test results. The 
tester performs the electrical measurements, while the handler ensures consistent 
mechanical and thermal conditions.

The process begins with packaged chips loaded into the handler in trays. After temperature 
conditioning, the handler transfers each chip into a test socket connected to the tester 
through a load board. Once electrical contact is established, the tester applies signals and 
measures the chip’s response. For ATC-equipped systems, a customised cold plate works 
with the active thermal control system to maintain the required chip temperature 
throughout testing. The handler then unloads and sorts the chips based on their test 
results.

Semiconductor test handler: workflow and thermal control system

Source: Hon Precision, Daiwa research

Handlers are used in both final test (FT) and system-level test (SLT). FT handlers work 
with automated test equipment (ATE) to check electrical characteristics and functionality 
against specifications. SLT handlers place chips into test positions connected to system-
level platforms, typically comprising test boards, test PCs and power supplies, to evaluate 
operation under conditions resembling actual use.

### Page 18

18

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Semiconductor back-end testing FT workflow

Source: Semiconductor engineering

Thermal control becomes increasingly important as AI/HPC chips consume more power 
and adopt larger, more complex packages. Different test patterns change power 
consumption and heat distribution, requiring dynamic heating or cooling to keep the chip 
within a specified temperature range. Precise handling and temperature control support 
repeatable measurements, while parallel testing and efficient chip movement help improve 
overall test throughput.

based platforms and comparable margin profiles. While industry-wide component

shortages persist, particularly in memory, CPUs and PCBs, management indicated
that tier-1 CSPs continue to secure sufficient supply.
Lower NB volumes and rising costs pressure margins: Quanta’s NB shipments fell
17% q/q, to 9.6M units in 3Q26, weaker than normal seasonality but above guidance
of a 20% decline. Rising memory costs and lower scale weighed on profitability, and
we expect margin pressure to persist in 4Q26. Management guides for another

double-digit q/q shipment decline in 4Q26, albeit narrower than in 3Q26, implying a
15–20% y/y decline for CY26. We project 4Q26 shipments of 8.3M units (-14% q/q)
and CY26 shipments of 39.4M units (-15% y/y). For CY27, management expects NB
shipments to decline in high single digits to 10% y/y and PC/NB revenue to fall to a
single-digit share of the mix, reflecting AI server racks’ substantially higher ASPs.
Keep price target at NT$385 and stay OW: Our target implies 11.8x CY27e P/E or

11.0x CY28e P/E, vs. its three-year range of 6-18x. Risk-reward looks attractive at  9.1x
CY27e P/E, below the three-year average of 11.7x, with ~5.1% dividend yield (DPS of
NT$15.6).

### Page 4

M
Update
4
3Q26 Results Summary
Component cost pressure drove 3Q GM miss:
Revenue was NT$1,115.5bn (+8% q/q, +125% y/y), but GM deteriorated to 4.8% (-30bps q/
q, -210bps y/y), which is 40bps below our estimate and 20bps below the Street.
Management cited the  following reasons for the GM miss:
• PC/NB: cost pressures from inflationary component pricing; diminishing economies
of scale.
• Servers: inflationary component pricing pressured server gross margins, despite a

slight decline in AI server shipments.
However, opex was also higher than expected, mainly owing to higher R&D expenses.
Thus, OP came in at NT$32.2bn (-6% q/q, +75% y/y), 7%  below our estimate and 112%
below the Street.
OpM was 2.9% (-40bps q/q, -80bps y/y). Management guided for 2H OpM to be flat h/h,
so we think 3Q OpM was a miss.
Non-op income was higher than expected because of ~NT$3.9bn in FX gains, so net profit
came in at ~NT$28.4bn (-1% q/q, +73% y/y) for EPS of NT$7.36, 6% above our estimate

and 2% above the Street.
Consignment? Under the buy-and-sell model, high-ASP server rack procurement inflated
revenue but diluted gross margins and to a lesser extent operating margins (owing to
operating leverage) via pass-through component accounting. Management continues to
stress absolute profit expansion and sustained >20% ROIC over margin ratios. To mitigate
balance sheet inventory carrying risk and working capital burden, Quanta will continue to

negotiate for a consignment business model with its CSP customers into CY27.

Estimate Changes
We update our model to factor in the 3Q26 margin miss and updated guidance from
management, which leads to increases of 7%, 13%, and 13% changes to our EPS estimates
for 2026, 2027, and 2028, respectively. We primarily raise our revenue assumptions while
lowering our margin assumptions for 2026-2028.

### Page 6

M
Update
6
Price Target and Valuation Methodology
We keep our price target at NT$385, reflecting the offset between raised 2026-28
earnings estimates and a lower medium-term growth rate: This implies 11.8x CY27e and
11.0x CY28e EPS, in line with the average of Quanta's three-year historical range of 11.7x.
We view this as reasonable because we expect the company to benefit from rising AI
server and EV demand over the long term, which will offset the weakness in the PC

segment and drive mix improvements.
We continue to apply a residual income model to derive our price target. Our key
assumptions include:
• A cost of equity of 9.0% (beta of 1.2, equity premium of 6.0%, and risk-free rate of
1.5%)
• A 7.0% medium-term growth rate – lowered from 8.5% previously, as we bake in
some conservatism on concerns over a potential slowdown in data center build-
out following the US midterm elections (see our Global Team's view), which could

affect Quanta's server business directly.
• A 3% terminal growth rate
Our bull and bear case scenario values also stay unchanged at $545 and NT$230,
respectively.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

We hosted NYPCB’s 3Q26 post result on November 9th. Key 
takeaways include: 
(1) NYPCB mentioned its 4Q26 GM on ABF substrate already 
exceeds 40% (vs. ~15% in 2025 and 25% in 1H26) driven mainly by 
the solid high-end product demand (which contributed 50%+ of the 
ABF revenue in 2026 vs. <20% in 2025), while the company 
mentioned it will gain more high-end market share in 2027 which 
should further drive company’s overall GM level.  
(2) For the new high-end projects in 2027, NYPCB mentioned it will 
gain significant share in the key US CSP next-gen AI project, and 
high-end server CPU project. Also, the company mentioned it 
expects a proactive adoption on 1.6T Ethernet switch demand 
driven by CSPs investment in AI infrastructure, driving its high-end 
switch substrate ASP (the upgrade on substrate, from 800G to 1.6T, 
suggesting a 50%+ higher substrate unit price, per GSe).  
(3) In terms of the ABF substrate pricing, NYPCB believes its ABF 
substrate ASP will further go up by 10-15% QoQ in 4Q26, while it 
also expects the pricing hike ratio to be at least maintained at this 
level from 1Q27-4Q27, without seeing much downside risk. 
Moreover, the company believes the ASP growth could be even 
faster than its expectation if the high-end orders accelerate in 
coming quarters or if they see an even tigher S/D outlook for the 
overall ABF substrate industry.  
(4) In terms of the BT substrate pricing outlook, the company 
mentioned the pricing hike ratio will be low single digits % QoQ in 
4Q26, which will further trend up in 1Q27-4Q27, with a high 
confidence level on the shortage of BT substrate in coming quarters.  
(4) In terms of 4Q26 revenue guidance, the company is expecting 
~20% QoQ revenue growth, driven mainly by the high-end ABF 
projects ramp up + new AI project ramps + BT substrate pricing hike

### Page 3

(low single digits % QoQ); moreover, the company expects its 1Q27 revenue to be flat 
QoQ, despite the ~20% less working days. For GM, the company expects 4Q26 GM to be 
low to mid 30% which should further improve in 1Q27.  
(5) The company mentioned it is working on a new capacity investment plan for 2028, 
but has not yet finalized the plan. In terms of the funding method, NYPCB mentioned it 
will not do any equity based fundraising, but will rely on internal cash or bank loan.  
(6) NYPCB also mentioned it will focus only on CoPoS technology, whose substrate 
pricing is also US$1000+ (vs. US$100-200 for the most high end ABF substrate now), 
while the product demand could start ramping up from 2028 and start contributing 
significant revenue from 2029. For EMIB-T, NYPCB believes the penetration rate could 
only be 10% in the foreseeable future, and it has no plan to invest in this small market.  
We maintain our positive view on the overall ABF substrate industry, with 34%/51% 
shortage ratio in 2027/28 (see here), and believe such high shortage ratio will protect 
companies’ GM/OPM even if we start to see some slowdown on AI CAPEX in coming 
years. We started to see all ABF substrate players’ GM surpass 40% in 4Q26, which is 
much faster than our original expectation (Taiwan ABF substrate suppliers reach 40%+ 
GM at the 6-7th quarters of the shortage in last upcycle, but it only takes 3-4 quarters 
for Taiwan players to hit the 40%+ GM, driven mainly by the solid AI upgrading demand 
this time). We expect NYPCB’s ABF substrate GM could reach historical peak level 
(55-60%) in 2H27, suggesting a fruitful upside for the EPS growth in coming years (we 
expect NYPCB’s EPS to grow by 150%+ 2025-29E CAGR), and believe the ABF substrate 
will continue to be a key bottleneck of the overall semi/tech supply chain in the next 3-5 
years.  
Maintain Buy on NYPCB, as the key spot price ABF substrate supplier in the market 
which should benefit strongly from the S/D shortage and pricing hike in the 
foreseeable future, with an unchanged TP of NT$2,700 (based on 21x 2028E EPS).  
Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - NYPCB 
NYPCB manufactures and markets printed circuit boards (PCBs) and integrated circuit 
(IC) substrates (including ABF and BT substrates). We are Buy rated on NYPCB 
considering the company’s high exposure to BT substrates and non-LTA ABF substrate 
(70%+ of total revenue), where we expect GM/OPM to increase significantly in the 
coming quarters (from breakeven in OPM in 1H25 to 26/42% in 2026/27E) due to the 
favorable substrate pricing outlook. Despite the company’s low exposure to high-end 
ABF substrate industry (<20% of total revenue in 2025), we believe the expanding 
demand from ASIC AI server and high-end switch IC demand will drive the company’s 
high-end ABF revenue exposure to 40%+ by 2027E, suggesting better GM/OPM outlook 
in the long term. In 2H25-2H26, we believe NYPCB will continue to benefit from stronger 
ABF/BT pricing and should be a key beneficiary of the pricing uptrend considering the 
company’s key focus on BT and non-LTA ABF substrate industries. Moreover, in the long 
term (after 2026), we expect the ABF industry to return to supply tightness, and NYPCB’s 
expanding high-end exposure will lead to a long-term earnings growth outlook (148% 
2026-28E earnings CAGR). As a result, we see solid upside potential for NYPCB’s

Price Target Risks and Methodology - NYPCB
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$2,700 for NYPCB is based
on 21x 2028E P/E, which is +1.5x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E (13.3x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, and (3)
Slower-than-expected NYPCB new high-end capacity qualification progress.

個股報告 
群聯(8299) 
AI 需求未減，營收規模持續擴大 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $2150 
前次投資建議：2026.08.14： 
「買進」 ，目標價: $4000

報告日期：2026.11.09 研 究 員：王穎

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 看好 AI及企業級相關產品需求維持強勁，且AI 產品組合佔比將進一步擴大，帶動營收規模
擴大，維持「買進」投資評等，然低價庫存利益將逐季下滑導致毛利率下降，因此下修目
標價至 2150 元(4 x 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 43.3%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/11/6 收盤價： 1,500.00 ➢ 26Q3 營收 860.76 億元，QoQ+26.8%，YoY+374.6%，
毛利率 59.9%，費用 184.89 億元，QoQ+5.25 億元，業
外獲利 30.77 億元，稅後淨利305.46 億元，EPS 138 元。
受惠產品組合轉向企業級及 AI 應用，使營收規模擴大，
然手機、PC 等消費性需求受到 NAND 漲價下出貨量下
滑，但群聯本身 controller 出貨量及營收仍提升，主要因
市占率提升，同時低價庫存利益將減少導致毛利率下滑。 
➢ 展望 26Q4 預期 NAND Falsh 報價持續提升且企業級及
AI相關之產品需求維持強勁，預期AI Ecosystem module
將持續提升，預期營收續創新高，然庫存成本提高使毛利
率下滑。因此兆豐預估營收 991.6 億元，QoQ+15.2%，
YoY+334.9%，毛利率 56.4%，稅後淨利 308.22 億元，
EPS為 139.3元。 
➢ 展望 2026~2027 年，預期至 2027 仍呈現供不應求之態
勢，公司坐擁大量庫存， 且群聯產品組合轉向 AI 
Ecosystem module 有助於有利營收規模持續放大，然隨
著庫存成本持續提升，預期毛利率將下滑。因此兆豐預估
營收 2940.9/4442.2 億元，YoY+304.7/51.1%，毛利率

營 運 分 析

群聯為企業級 SSD領導廠商 
群聯電子股份有限公司成立於 2000 年 11月 8 日，為國內NAND FLASH控
制 IC 廠，主要業務為 NAND FLASH 控制 IC及周邊系統產品的研發設計製
造及銷售。公司已經成為USB隨身碟、SD記憶卡、eMMC、UFS、PATA、
SATA 與 PCIe 固態磁碟等控制晶片領域的領頭廠。 26Q3 產品組合 AI 
Ecosystem Module 57%、 Embedded ODM Module  24%、 Industrial

- 3 - 
個股報告 
26Q3產品組合轉佳帶動營收會模擴大 
26Q3 營收 860.76 億元，QoQ+26.8%，YoY+374.6%，毛利率 59.9%，費
用 184.89 億元，QoQ+5.25 億元，業外獲利 30.77 億元，稅後淨利 305.46
億元，EPS 138 元。受惠產品組合轉向企業級及AI應用，使營收規模擴大，
然手機、 PC 等消費性需求受到 NAND 漲價下出貨量下滑，但群聯本身
controller 出貨量及營收仍提升，主要因市占率提升，預期待消費性及工業市
場歷經庫存調整後，有望逐步恢復。同時低價庫存利益將減少導致毛利率下
滑。26Q3 庫存金額 1117.7億元，較 26Q2 增加 199.8 億元，存貨週轉天數
由 297天下降至 274 天，存貨主要配置於企業級、AI應用及 boot drive 等應
用，以因應後續客戶需求。

26Q4預期營收規模擴大獲利持續創高 
展望 26Q4預期 NAND Falsh報價持續提升，於 main drive持續取得不同客
戶 design win，main drive 對於 NAND容量需求更大，於 26Q3 取得多項新
專案 ， 預期26Q4~27Q1將持續增加 。 同時PCIe Gen6企業級 SSD預期 2026
年 11月底開始提供樣品，與 CPU 業者共同發布相關 solution，主要鎖定 AI
推論相關應用，因此企業級及 AI相關之產品需求將於 2027 年維持強勁，預
期 AI Ecosystem module 將持續提升，預期營收續創新高，然庫存成本提高
使毛利率下滑。因此兆豐預估營收991.6 億元，QoQ+15.2%，YoY+334.9%，
毛利率 56.4%，稅後淨利 308.22 億元，EPS為 139.3元。

圖三、NAND歷年供需比

資料來源：兆豐投顧整理

2026 NAND Flash供需缺口逐步擴大 
NAND FLASH 市場呈現明顯的週期性循環，每約 3.5～4年進入一個完整供
需週期，2026 NAND Flash 呈現供不應求，且下半年供需缺口擴大，有利報
價穩定甚至持續上漲。 同時 Nvidia ICMS (Inference Context Memory 
Storage) 的快取層級架構，用於解決大型語言模型推論時的記憶體瓶頸，推
升 NAND需求 ， 提升Token吞吐量 5 倍 、 推論延遲顯著下降 、 能效優化5 倍。
預期 2027 年用於 Nvidia KV Cache之 NAND 需求將達總需求約 10%。進一
步造成供需缺口擴大。

- 5 - 
個股報告 
預期 AI佔比提升帶動營收規模放大 
2026~2027 年，預期至 2027 仍呈現供不應求之態勢，公司坐擁大量庫存，
main drive、PCIe Gen6 等產品預期至 2027 年都將持續成長使群聯產品組合
轉向 AI Ecosystem module 有助於有利營收規模持續放大，另外在消費性市
場持續耕耘，取得更高手機、PC市占率，Client SSD方面，2026 年已取得
6 個 PCIe Gen5 TLC 及 QlC controller 專案，未來將協助原廠開發相容
aiDAPTIV之 Client SSD方案，結合 AI PC於記憶體擴充需求，擴大 PC市
場影響力，因此出貨量將持續增加，待消費性庫存歷經調整後，出貨量將進
一步提升，然隨著庫存成本持續提升，預期毛利率將下滑。因此兆豐預估營
收 2940.9/4442.2 億元，YoY+304.7/51.1%，毛利率 60.1/51.7%，稅後淨利
1027.6/1187.7億元，YoY+1075.8/15.6%，EPS為 464.7/536.9 元。

看好 AI相關產品組合佔比提升，維持「買進」投資評等 
群聯目前位於 2.8 X 27(F)PER)，看好 AI及企業級相關產品需求維持強勁，
且 AI產品組合佔比將進一步擴大，維持「買進」投資評等，然低價庫存利益
將逐季下滑導致毛利率下降，因此下修目標價至 2150 元(4 x 27(F)PER)，潛
在上漲空間 43.3%。

圖五 群聯(8299) P/E Band

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025.

Biểu đồ 1. Kết quả kinh doanh theo quý  Biểu đồ 2. Diễn biến doanh thu thuần/ngày và doanh thu bán 
lẻ/ngày theo quý

Nguồn: PNJ, KBSV   Nguồn: PNJ, KBSV

### Page 2

2

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T 
 — PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ.

Phương án bổ sung vốn và tái cơ cấu hoạt động kinh doanh 
— Đại hội thông qua phương án chào bán tối đa 550 triệu cổ phiếu. Nguồn vốn dự kiến 
phân bổ 60% cho kế hoạch kinh doanh gắn với điều chỉnh chính sách thu đổi, 20% bổ 
sung vốn lưu động và phần còn lại bổ sung vốn lưu động và/hoặc trả vay ngắn hạn. 
Nhu cầu vốn Q4/2026 và Q1/2027 lần lượt khoảng 3,100 tỷ VND và 2,800 tỷ VND. Giá và 
khối lượng phát hành thực tế chưa được xác định tại đại hội.  
— Doanh nghiệp dự kiến đóng 25–30 cửa hàng và mở khoảng 10 cửa hàng (có chọn lọc) 
trong năm 2026, tương ứng giảm ròng 15–20 cửa hàng; đồng thời đặt mục tiêu giảm 
20–30% chi phí nhân sự. Cổ tức tiền mặt năm 2026 được điều chỉnh về 0%; cổ tức 2025 
về 10% mệnh giá và đã chi trả. 
— 100% mẫu kim cương được AIGS và Vinacontrol kiểm định độc lập là kim cương thiên 
nhiên, phù hợp thông số 4C. PNJ dự kiến trang bị máy kiểm định tại các cửa hàng trọng 
điểm để khách hàng kiểm tra trực tiếp.

Đánh giá của KBSV 
— KBSV cho rằng huy động vốn từ phát hành riêng lẻ là phương án phù hợp ở thời điểm 
hiện tại trong bối cảnh lãi suất tăng cao, giúp PNJ cân đối thanh khoản và duy trì hoạt 
động liên tục. Tuy nhiên, với khối lượng phát hành lớn và chưa có phương án chi tiết, 
doanh nghiệp sẽ cần thêm thời gian để hoàn thành phương án phát hành và cân đối 
giữa lợi ích của cổ đông và doanh nghiệp.  
— Nhu cầu yếu tại Trung Quốc và cạnh tranh từ kim cương nuôi cấy giá thấp tiếp tục gây 
áp lực lên giá kim cương tự nhiên, khiến PNJ gặp khó khăn trong việc tiêu thụ tồn kho 
nhóm kim cương  và gia tăng tổn thất khi mua lại theo chính sách cũ. Thực tế, tỷ trọng 
kim cương và trang sức kim cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% cuối Q2/2026 lên 
52% cuối Q3/2026, với giá trị ước khoảng 9,000 tỷ VND. KBSV cho rằng cần theo dõi (1) 
nhu cầu thị trường trong giai đoạn cuối năm; (2) tốc độ giảm tồn kho và dòng tiền thu 
về từ bán hàng; và (3) giá trị khách hàng bán lại. Diễn biến các yếu tố này sẽ quyết định 
khả năng thu hồi vốn, mức độ phục hồi biên lợi nhuận và nhu cầu trích lập bổ sung 
trong quá trình xử lý. 
— Ước tính bán niên sử dụng tỷ lệ tổn thất 20.3% đối với hàng bán trong giai đoạn 2020–
2026 dự kiến được thu mua lại, thấp hơn mức 28.9% trên các giao dịch mua lại thực tế 
từ 01/07–03/09/2026. Vì vậy, bên cạnh giả định thu đổi 80%, mức tổn thất khi xử lý 
hàng mua lại là yếu tố quan trọng cần theo dõi. Việc chuyển hạch toán dự phòng từ 
SG&A sang giá vốn tại kỳ soát xét bán niên cũng cho thấy áp lực biên lợi nhuận bị co 
hẹp trong quá trình tái cấu trúc về mặt kế toán.

Historical day last Friday in Taiwan with index down close to 10% before making a 2k

points climb intraday.  Index futures also touched limit down briefly - last time it
happened was during Jul'26 degrossing. Due to such volatility, FSC has decided to
intervene the market. The Desk stayed back on Friday to put out below notes. Special
thanks to our trading desk head Elaine Kuo, sales Jill Huang, and compliance Daniel
Lin.
Taiwan’s NATIONAL STABILIZATION FUND held a meeting in the afternoon
[2026/11/6] and agreed to activate the National Stabilization Fund (NSF) with

NT$500b or US$15.2b usable capital to stabilize the mkt. NSF had a good track
record in its previous 9 interventions. The mkt rallied 6% on avg in the following week
after announcement, and +22% over one year. However, 6 out of 9 times experienced
additional 5%~28% drawdowns within 6 months after activation.
While National Stabilization Fund announcement provides a sentiment boost to the
stock market, the actual deployed capital could be limited - only NT$0.76b~122.7b

(US$23m~3.7b) were actually deployed in the past 9 instances vs. NSF NT$500b
(US$15.2b) usable capital. That being said, from previous experience, NSF tends to
buy large cap tech/ financials with heavy index weighting (see below).

Furthermore, Taiwan's Financial Supervisory Commission (FSC) announced five
market stabilization measures effective November 9, 2026, without specifying an
end date, to calm stock market volatility following sharp selloffs in the Taiwan Market:
1. Intraday Short-Selling Cap: Lowered the daily short sell limit from 30% to 3% of
30-day average daily trading volume (exemptions apply to hedging and market-
making activities).
2. Margin Requirement Hike: Increased the minimum margin requirement for

retails’ margin short selling listed stocks (融券保證⾦成數) from 90% to 130%.
11/9/26, 8:28 AM GS SALESTRADING: Taiwan National Stabilization Fund and Tighter Short Sell Restriction | GSTW
https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/18f8509b-7aa3-4cdb-8093-13ae5c123cb2 2/9

### Page 3

3. Expanded Collateral Scope: Allowed investors to use alternative liquid collateral
(such as foreign securities held via sub-brokerage accounts) to meet margin calls
and cover financing shortfalls.
4. Encourage Share Buybacks: Urged listed companies to initiate treasury share
buybacks based on their financial and capital conditions.
5. Encourage Investor Communications: Encouraged listed companies to host
institutional investor conferences and earnings presentations to reinforce

corporate fundamentals.
These measures are very similar to last time in 2025 April. Some investors concern
that lowered daily short sell limit (for institutional investors’ short selling) and higher
margin requirement (for retail investors’ margin short selling) will hurt market
participation and liquidity.

realize each day a loss or a gain which is a multiple (for leveraged ETFs and ETNs) or the inverse (for inverse ETFs and ETNs) of the

performance on that day of their underlying index or benchmark. For this reason, if they are held for a period longer than one day, their
performance over such longer periods of time can differ significantly from the stated multiple of the performance (or the inverse of the
performance) of their underlying index or benchmark during the same period of time. This effect can be magnified in volatile markets.
Prior to entering into a transaction in leveraged or inverse ETFs, you should be aware of the general risks associated with such

transactions. You should not enter into leveraged or inverse ETFs transactions unless you understand the nature and extent of your risk
exposure. You should also be satisfied that the leveraged or inverse ETFs transaction is appropriate for you in light of your circumstances
and financial condition. ETFs and ETNs that are linked to commodity futures do not offer direct exposure to the commodity's spot price,
and may perform differently than the spot price for the commodity itself. Performance differential can be magnified if a specific

condition persists in the market for a commodity that creates a disparity between near-term future prices and long-term future prices
and may lead to unexpected performance results. Other factors, such as roll yield, transaction costs, management fees, and taxes may
cause deviation in performance between the spot price of a commodity and commodity futures. You should not assume that an ETF or
ETN that is linked to commodity futures will provide an effective hedge because of a negative correlation with equities or other asset

−17.0%（加權 −7.1%）：市場只區分出 ASIC。

事件
創辦⼈在董事會調查與國會壓⼒下辭職，財務⻑暫代，正式⼈選由外部顧問協助遴選
兩週內由「董事會⽀持」轉為辭職：先是外國情報⾏動之報導，後是與屬下關係之報導與國會壓⼒
10 ⽉ 29 ⽇（美東時間，下同）媒體報導美國情報評估，⼀名晶⽚買家之主管把資訊傳回北京、並與⿈仁勳關係密切；11
⽉ 4 ⽇眾議院三位委員會主席致函邀其出席閉⾨簡報，11 ⽉ 6 ⽇媒體再報導政府備忘錄認定該⼈為中國情報⼈員。11 ⽉ 8
⽇董事會宣布由獨⽴董事委員會調查，表⽰沒有理由相信國家安全或輝達機密遭到危害、仍信任其領導；⿈仁勳同⽇致信
員⼯承認對婚姻不忠。11 ⽉ 10 ⽇獨⽴董事委任前紐約南區代理聯邦檢察官主持調查；同⽇有媒體報導相關公司網絡協助
⾄少 75 億美元之輝達晶⽚流⼊中國，此數字未經其他媒體證實，輝達先前亦稱相關轉運指控已查明不實。11 ⽉ 11 ⽇兩黨
議員陸續要求其辭職，晚間媒體報導其與⼀位直屬⾼階主管之關係（輝達未回應），董事會當晚開會並接受辭呈，11 ⽉ 12
⽇上午（台北時間 21:52）以 8-K 公告。

治理結構同步調整：⾸度設⽴董事⻑，暫代執⾏⻑兼任財務⻑
⿈仁勳辭去總裁、執⾏⻑與董事，公司發⾔⼈表⽰其⽇後不以任何形式參與輝達。財務⻑ Colette Kress（2013 年加⼊，
曾任 Cisco 與 Microsoft 事業部財務主管）暫代執⾏⻑並續任財務⻑；1993 年起擔任董事、曾任 Synopsys 執⾏⻑之
Harvey C. Jones 出任輝達⾸任董事⻑（輝達過去不設董事⻑）。董事會將考慮內外部⼈選並委請外部公司協助，未公布時
程。第三季財報維持 11 ⽉ 17 ⽇公布；據眾議院外交委員會主席表⽰，⿈仁勳同意於感恩節前赴國會作證。

2027 年以前
2027 年之出貨⼤致鎖定：產品已量產、產能已預付、承諾已簽訂
Vera Rubin 已量產出貨，主要出貨量在 2027 年；Rubin Ultra 以前之規格已定案
輝達 8 ⽉表⽰ Vera Rubin 於當⽉開始量產出貨，管理層預估約占第三季資料中⼼營收兩成；鴻海表⽰ Vera Rubin 第四季
放量、為明年主⼒，廣達表⽰第四季⼩量、2027 年第⼀季放量。管理層 8 ⽉預估 2028 會計年度（約當 2027 年 2 ⽉⾄
2028 年 1 ⽉）營收成⻑約七成，屬供給受限之數字；3 ⽉ GTC 時稱 Blackwell 與 Vera Rubin ⾄ 2027 年之訂單能⾒度⾄少

1 兆美元。據供應鏈訪查，Rubin Ultra 與 Feynman 之開發已進⼊執⾏階段；新任管理層第⼀個能實質調整的，是
Feynman 世代之規格、代⼯與封裝分配。
供應商受合約保護：2,790 億美元之採購與產能承諾，加上客⼾之預付款
輝達 7 ⽉底之季報揭露表外之供應與產能採購承諾 2,790 億美元（前⼀季 1,190 億美元），主要為記憶體與製造設施，台積
電、HBM 廠與主要組裝廠 2027 年之產能多已被預訂。下游⽅⾯，廣達 11 ⽉ 11 ⽇法說會表⽰，迄今未⾒客⼾調整訂單，
客⼾若延後拉貨需負擔相關利息與費⽤；以 CoreWeave 為例，其積壓訂單約 1,325 億美元，未來三個⽉另有約 90 億美元
之客⼾預付款與押⾦，綁定的是機房與晶⽚。
近期之⼲擾來⾃轉換期與出⼝管制，⽽⾮需求
據供應鏈訪查，第四季 VR200、Trainium 3 與 TPU v8 之新平台轉換均因零組件與組裝良率⽽遞延，屬時程問題。較需留
意的是出⼝管制：據報美國商務部正敲定新規，把⾺來西亞、新加坡等 7 國納⼊較嚴格之限制，涵蓋 Blackwell 以上晶⽚

及遠端使⽤；和碩 11 ⽉ 12 ⽇法說會表⽰此類限制並⾮新規，已納⼊法務審核。國會亦把晶⽚追蹤（Chip Security Act）
與出⼝許可審查（AI Overwatch Act）列為國防授權法之候選條⽂。本次事件涉及外國情報與晶⽚流向之指控，調查結果
出爐前，管制收緊之機率已上升，台灣組裝廠出貨東南亞機房之合規成本將提⾼。
接班情境
三種接班路徑對產品節奏、供應商議價與客⼾融資之影響不同；ToAlpha 以內部技術主
管接任為基本情境
董事會之⾸要任務是穩定客⼾與⼯程團隊，內部⼈接任之機率最⾼
據報導與預測市場，內部⼈選以 CUDA 創始⼈、現負責⼤型雲端與 HPC 業務之 Ian Buck 呼聲最⾼，其次為暫代之
Kress；技術⻑ Michael Kagan、⾸席科學家 Bill Dally 與 GPU ⼯程主管 Jonah Alben 亦被提及。外部⼈選包括曾任職輝
3 / 12

### Page 4

達七年之 Arm 執⾏⻑ Rene Haas；Apple 硬體主管 Johny Srouji 據報亦在考慮之列，美國總統則據報公開推薦前 Apple
執⾏⻑ Tim Cook。全球業務主管 Jay Puri 已於 6 ⽉宣布退休。輝達沒有公開之接班計畫、約 60 位主管直接向⿈仁勳報
告；正式⼈選產⽣前由 Kress 暫代，市場預期約⼀年。ToAlpha 以內部技術主管接任為基本情境（45%），營運財務型（含
Kress 續任或外部營運型）30%，外部產業執⾏⻑ 25%。
無論何⼈接任，新增融資與規格升級之積極程度都可能低於⿈仁勳時代
輝達 7 ⽉底持有⾮上市股權約 512 億美元、上市股權約 428 億美元，另有最⾼ 1,085 億美元之保證與 250 億美元之股權投
資承諾；運算承諾⽅⾯，OpenAI 規劃約 12GW 之輝達運算、Anthropic 最多 1GW。已簽承諾會延續，但新增部分之審核
標準可能提⾼：據報 Kress 曾反對為 OpenAI 提供最⾼ 1,050 億美元之擔保；市場亦期待新任管理層提⾼現⾦回饋、回應

「循環融資」之疑慮。規格升級⽅⾯，據供應鏈訪查，⿈仁勳之產品策略對供應鏈有「產能預訂」與「規格升級」兩項激
進影響，我們認為這兩項也是輝達供應鏈過去享有較⾼評價之重要原因。新任管理層若偏重成本與執⾏穩定，800V
HVDC、共同封裝光學、M9 等級銅箔基板等升級之導⼊可能放慢，供應鏈成本管理亦會更嚴格。

資料來源：ToAlpha。⼈選為媒體報導與預測市場所列，⾮公司公布；機率為 ToAlpha 主觀判斷，將隨董事會遴選進度調整。
產品路線
Feynman 以前之路線圖已進⼊執⾏，新任管理層可調整之處在決策速度與規格升級
路線圖本⾝未變，但 2026 年已出現取捨：Rubin CPX 擱置、Kyber 機櫃傳延⾄ 2028 年
Rubin 為兩顆光罩極限尺⼨晶粒、每顆搭載 288GB HBM4 之設計[5]，量產爬坡已受 HBM4 規格與良率牽制。輝達今年並
把原訂 2026 年底之 Rubin CPX 移出路線圖，改以 Groq 授權技術之 LPX 推論機櫃處理解碼；產業研究機構 7 ⽉指出
Kyber 機櫃因 PCB 中背板之可製造性延⾄ 2028 年，輝達回應路線圖不變。這顯⽰即使在⿈仁勳任內，年更節奏已觸及供

應鏈之物理極限。我們認為新任管理層較可能之調整，是把年更改為「⼤世代兩年、中間做強化版」，並集中資源於推論
（LPX、Vera CPU）與網路（Spectrum-X、共同封裝光學）。
開放策略可能加速：NVLink Fusion 讓 ASIC 客⼾也採購輝達元件
NVLink Fusion 已納⼊ AWS Trainium4、Marvell 客製 XPU 與 Intel 客製 x86 CPU，輝達 3 ⽉並⼊股 Marvell 20 億美元。
對新任管理層⽽⾔，與其與 ASIC 正⾯競爭，不如在 ASIC 機櫃內銷售 CPU、網卡、交換器與 NVLink；輝達之營收來源因
此分散，但 GPU 本⾝之份額讓渡也會更快。對台灣供應鏈⽽⾔，交換器、網卡相關供應商不必選邊。

資料來源：輝達、鴻海、廣達、和碩、產業研究機構、據供應鏈訪查；ToAlpha 整理。
AMD∕博通∕ASIC
Google TPU 與 AMD 最直接受益，CoWoS 份額之移轉在事件前已開始
事件前之路徑：輝達占 CoWoS 需求由 2026 年 56% 降⾄ 2027 年 45%
據供應鏈訪查，2027 年全球 CoWoS 需求約 2,694 千⽚，輝達 1,222 千⽚、成⻑ 57%，⾮輝達合計成⻑ 140%；AMD 因
OpenAI、Meta 與 Anthropic 之 MI450 系列放量，份額由 9% 升⾄ 20%，聯發科（Google TPU）由 3% 升⾄ 7%。產業研
究機構亦預期 TPU 出貨 2027 年倍增以上，⾼於輝達⾼階 GPU 約三成之成⻑。換⾔之，輝達之份額下滑早於醜聞；接班不

確定性讓既有趨勢加速，⽽⾮創造新趨勢。
Google TPU 最直接：客⼾與供應鏈之信任轉移最快
據供應鏈訪查，在輝達⾯臨接班不確定性下，Google TPU 最可能先取得客⼾與供應鏈之進⼀步信任，設計夥伴博通與聯發
科最直接受惠。博通 9 ⽉表⽰ AI 營收 2026 會計年度約 580 億美元、2027 年約 1,150 億、2028 年約 2,300 億美元，六家
XPU 客⼾中 Anthropic 2027 年部署 5GW、2028 年再 10GW，OpenAI ⾃研推論晶⽚ 2027 年 1.3GW、2028 年逾 5GW。聯
發科表⽰第⼆顆 ASIC 按計畫於 2028 年量產。
AMD：具名承諾最多 14GW，2027 年起才⼤量部署，能否接住移轉取決於 Helios 機櫃之量產速度
AMD 第三季資料中⼼營收 85.5 億美元、年增 97%，MI455X 已開始量產出貨，具名承諾 OpenAI 6GW、Meta 6GW、
Anthropic 2GW。據供應鏈訪查，Helios 機櫃之⼤量出貨在 2027 年第⼆季。AMD 之 MI350 把 3 奈⽶運算晶粒 3D 堆疊於

6 奈⽶ I/O 晶粒之上[4]，MI450 系列據報再採 2 奈⽶運算晶粒、每顆 432GB HBM4，以單晶⽚規格⾒⻑，但機櫃級整合之
經驗仍少於輝達。輝達若在 2027 年邊際讓出份額，AMD 之瓶頸是 CoWoS 與 HBM 配額，⽽⾮客⼾意願。
⾦額份額與顆數份額差距⼤：ASIC 顆數 2028 年超越 GPU，⾦額仍以輝達為主
產業研究機構估計客製 ASIC 出貨顆數將於 2028 年超越資料中⼼ GPU；以⾦額計，據供應鏈訪查 ASIC 占加速器市場由約
⼀成升⾄ 2027 年約兩成。輝達 Vera Rubin 每 GW 營收約 400 億美元，博通估其每 GW 內容價值約 250 億美元（其估計之
中值）。份額移轉對輝達營收之影響因此⼩於對出貨顆數之影響；但對依顆數與機櫃數計價之台灣組裝與零組件供應商，影
響較接近顆數。
技術差距縮⼩：AMD 與 ASIC 之單晶⽚規格追近，輝達之優勢轉向機櫃整合與軟體
以廠商公布之稠密峰值計，AMD MI455X 之 FP4 約 40 PFLOPS、Rubin 約 35 PFLOPS[2]（輝達官⽅標⽰之 50 PFLOPS 為

NVFP4 推論值）；Google TPU 歷經五代維持架構穩定，以規模與能效取勝[1]。輝達之差異化愈來愈落在 NVLink 機櫃整
合、網路與 CUDA ⽣態，⽽這些正是需要領導⼈跨部⾨拍板之領域；接班空窗期之⾵險，在於競爭者單晶⽚規格追近之
際，輝達之系統整合節奏放慢。
5 / 12

### Page 6

ToAlpha 份額移轉之推導：融資收斂與客⼾分散下單，2027 年約 2、2028 年約 4 個百分點
2027 年：明年約四分之⼀業務需輝達融資⽀撐，若新增融資縮減⼀⾄⼆成，約為輝達量 2.5%〜5%，以 45% 份額換算約
1〜2 個百分點，加上客⼾邊際分散下單，取 2 個百分點（1.5〜2.5）。2028 年：融資依賴之需求縮減約兩成（約輝達量
5%），加上⼤型客⼾新專案約 4% 轉向 AMD 與 ASIC，合計約輝達量 9%、約 4 個百分點（3〜5）。輝達減少之量，2027 年
七成、2028 年⼋成由 AMD 與 ASIC 依原份額吸收，其餘為依賴輝達融資、隨之消失之需求，CoWoS 總量因此少約 0.6%、
0.8%。供應商營收以平台出貨量⽐例估算，未區分每⽚晶圓之價值差異。

之⽐重為 ToAlpha 依法說會說法取整之假設，個別公司可能與組別平均差異明顯；台積電與⽇⽉光投控以 CoWoS 總量計。影響為 AI 營收，⾮淨利。
韓國∕中國∕美國
台灣份額 2027 年前難以外移；韓國之⾓⾊在⿈仁勳任內已擴⼤，美國是⽣產地點之轉
移，中國沒有承接能⼒
物理限制：輝達之 CoWoS 約九成在台灣封裝，美國之 CoWoS 級產能最早 2028 年初
據供應鏈訪查，輝達 2027 年約 1,222 千⽚之 CoWoS 需求中，約 132 千⽚由 Amkor 承接，其餘由台積電封裝。美國⽅

⾯，Amkor 亞利桑那廠 2028 年初量產、台積電亞利桑那封裝廠尚無 CoWoS 量產時程、SK 海⼒⼠印第安那 HBM 封裝廠
6 / 12

### Page 7

2029 年、三星泰勒⼆廠 2030 年；亞利桑那⽣產之 Blackwell 晶圓⽬前仍須運回台灣封裝。因此 2027 年以前之 Rubin 與
Rubin Ultra，前段晶圓與封裝⾼度依賴台灣；新任管理層第⼀個可調整之世代是 Feynman。
韓國：HBM 主要在韓國，增量在封裝與代⼯，且多為⿈仁勳任內之決定
據業界估計，Rubin 之 HBM4 由 SK 海⼒⼠供應約三分之⼆、三星約四分之⼀以上；產業研究機構 6 ⽉⼤幅上修三星之
HBM4 份額，三星今年亦發表單堆疊 36GB、3.3TB/s 之 HBM4[3]。台灣在 HBM 之⾓⾊主要為美光在台產能。韓國之增量
有兩處：推論晶⽚ LP30 交由三星 4 奈⽶；據報輝達 10 ⽉宣布與 Amkor ⾄ 2028 年底把韓國中介層產能提⾼ 300% 以上。
另據報輝達要求記憶體廠把客製 HBM 之 base die 交台積電代⼯，則對台灣有利。新任管理層若偏重成本與供應分散，韓
國在⾮旗艦產品之代⼯與封裝份額可能再提⾼，但旗艦 GPU 之前段 2028 年前仍無替代。

中國：管制只會收緊，台廠之⾵險在出貨中國客⼾與東南亞機房
輝達財測沿⽤不計⼊中國資料中⼼運算營收之做法；產業研究機構估計 2026 年上半年輝達在中國 AI 加速器之份額降⾄
34%、華為升⾄ 28%。本次事件涉及晶⽚流向中國之指控，新任管理層之合規只會更嚴，供應鏈不會流向中國；受影響的
是出貨中國客⼾或東南亞機房之台灣組裝廠，例如英業達 5 ⽉法說會表⽰其 AI 伺服器以中國客⼾占⽐偏⾼。
美國：地點轉移由台廠執⾏，營收仍在台灣公司，但成本較⾼
美國商務部⻑表⽰⽬標把台灣約四成半導體產能移⾄美國；輝達宣⽰在美國⽣產最多 5,000 億美元之 AI 基礎設施，緯創德
州廠已⽣產 GB300 並準備 Vera Rubin，鴻海休⼠頓廠興建中、規劃⽣產 GB300 運算模組。新任執⾏⻑在國安疑慮之政治
環境下，加速「美國製造」之誘因更⾼；但承接者仍是台積電、鴻海、緯創、廣達等台廠，屬地點轉移。美國本⼟之替代
以 Intel 與 Amkor 為主：據報 Feynman 之 I/O 晶粒與最多四分之⼀之最終封裝可能交 Intel，據供應鏈訪查 Google TPU

後續世代預計採 Intel EMIB-T 封裝；這類分散化之決定權在新任管理層⼿上。

台廠為主 美國廠多在興建 規格升級節奏放慢之影響⼤於份額
資料來源：輝達、台積電、Amkor、SK 海⼒⼠、三星、產業研究機構、據供應鏈訪查；ToAlpha 整理。
直接供應鏈
量之衝擊⼩於規格升級節奏之衝擊：2027 年之價值成⻑相當部分來⾃ Vera Rubin 之規
格
機櫃數之估計分歧，單櫃價值翻倍是共同看法
據供應鏈訪查，NVL72 機櫃出貨 2026 年約 82 千櫃、2027 年約 90 千櫃；產業研究機構則估計 2027 年成⻑逾五成，兩者
差距⼤。共同之看法是 Vera Rubin 系統平均售價約為 GB300 之兩倍，廣達亦表⽰新世代 GPU 伺服器單價⾼四到五成。
7 / 12

### Page 8

2027 年輝達供應鏈之成⻑相當部分來⾃單櫃價值，⽽單櫃價值來⾃規格升級，這正是新任管理層最可能放慢之部分。組裝
份額⽅⾯，鴻海由 2026 年約 37% 升⾄ 2027 年約 43%，廣達約 26%，緯創約 24%。
受影響之先後：規格升級受惠者最先，依機櫃數計價者次之，晶圓與封裝最後
800V HVDC 電源（台達電、光寶科）、共同封裝光學、M9 等級銅箔基板（台光電、聯茂）、液冷與雙⾯冷板（奇鋐、雙鴻）
等受惠於規格升級之供應商，其時點已隨 Kyber 傳延⾄ 2028 年；更替使「升級是否如期」之不確定性增加。依機櫃數計
價之組裝、滑軌、機構件次之。台積電與 CoWoS 因產能不⾜、可轉給 AMD 與 ASIC，受影響最⼩。另據供應鏈訪查，台灣
供應商將⾯臨更嚴格之價格談判，訂單亦可能進⼀步分散；新任管理層若以成本管理⾒⻑，台灣組裝廠本已偏薄之⽑利率
將承壓。
終端客⼾結構亦重要：neocloud 對輝達融資之依賴⾼於⼤型雲端業者
⼤型雲端業者以⾃有現⾦流與⻑約採購，neocloud 與前沿實驗室則較依賴輝達之⼊股、保底合約與擔保。若新任管理層收

緊新增融資，受影響之需求集中在 neocloud；和碩表⽰其伺服器⽬前以 neocloud 與⽩牌為主。同樣以輝達平台為主，客
⼾以⼤型雲端業者為主者（如廣達）較為穩定。
台股曝險
以輝達平台為主之台股：2027 年影響有限，不利處在缺少 ASIC 動能與 2028 年之份額
移轉
市場⽬前只區分出 ASIC：以 ASIC 為主者相對抗跌，純輝達與兩邊都有者差異不⼤
10 ⽉ 29 ⽇⾄ 11 ⽉ 12 ⽇，加權指數 −7.1%，32 家中以 ASIC 為主之 10 家平均 −9.4%、以輝達平台為主之 5 家 −14.0%、
兩邊都有之 17 家 −17.0%；11 ⽉ 12 ⽇傳出董事會準備撤換之當⽇，三組分別 −5.2%、−5.3%、−2.8%（加權 −2.6%）。此
期間同時有美國期中選舉後之科技股修正，兩邊都有之組別多為前波漲幅⼤之零組件股，跌幅亦⼤，個股差異不全來⾃本
事件。市場對純輝達者尚未額外折價，反映 2027 年之訂單能⾒度。
純輝達之台股：2027 年營收有保護，不利處在缺少 ASIC 動能與 2028 年之份額移轉

以輝達平台為主之五家，2027 年之 Vera Rubin 出貨已排定，單價提⾼四到五成，營收成⻑仍可期；ToAlpha 基本情境之
份額移轉僅使其 2027 年 AI 營收相對原本路徑少約 3.4%。不利之處有三：其⼀，缺少 2027〜2028 年成⻑最快之 ASIC 動
能；其⼆，2028 年份額移轉使其 AI 營收少約 6.7%，若規格升級放慢，單櫃價值之成⻑亦打折；其三，依賴輝達之配貨與
融資，新任管理層之成本與融資政策直接影響其⽑利與客⼾。廣達已表⽰⾮ GPU 平台 2027 年將升⾄雙位數、和碩有 4〜5
家 ASIC 客⼾將於 2027 年放量，組裝廠可逐步降低集中度；我們認為華碩、技嘉之伺服器業務較難轉向 ASIC，因雲端業者
⾃研晶⽚多直接委託 ODM。華碩、技嘉、和碩之⾮ AI 業務占⽐亦⾼，股價受 AI 以外因素影響⼤。緯創⺟公司以輝達運算
板為主，惟合併⼦公司緯穎後屬兩邊都有。
兩邊都有之供應商：份額移轉之影響約 −1%，⾵險在規格升級節奏
奇鋐表⽰「GPU 與 ASIC ⾮⼆選⼀、兩邊都出貨」；據供應鏈訪查，雙鴻之 ASIC 營收 2027 年可望約占四成。這類供應商在

份額移轉下之 AI 營收相對原本路徑少約 1.3%（2027 年）、2.3%（2028 年），其⾵險在規格升級之節奏，⽽⾮平台選擇。
台積電與⽇⽉光投控依 CoWoS 總量計，影響約 −0.6%、−0.8%。

⾵險
本報告之主要不確定性在於接班⼈選、調查結果與 CoWoS 產能是否仍不⾜
接班⼈選與調查結果：董事會調查與國會調查若發現更多出⼝管制問題，影響可能擴及輝達之營運許可，份額移轉將超
出本報告區間；反之若迅速確定具技術背景之接班⼈，2028 年之移轉可能⼩於基本情境。
產能與吸收假設：本報告假設 CoWoS 與 HBM 2027〜2028 年仍不⾜、輝達減少之量七⾄⼋成由 AMD 與 ASIC 吸收。若
產能轉為過剩或依賴融資之需求消失更多，份額移轉將伴隨總量下降，對台積電與兩邊都有之供應商亦不利。
分組與⽐重：各公司之輝達⽐重為依法說會說法之 ToAlpha 判斷，部分公司未揭露客⼾結構；供應商營收以平台出貨量

⽐例估算，未區分每⽚晶圓之價值。個別公司之實際影響可能與所屬分組平均有明顯差異。
股價區間：事件期間之股價差異同時受美國期中選舉後之科技股修正與個別公司消息影響，不宜全數歸因於本事件。

本報告由 ToAlpha（toalpha.tw）研究部編製，屬純產業研究，⾮投資建議。本報告僅描述產業、供應鏈與情境影響，不對任何個股提供評等、⽬標
價或買進、賣出、持有之建議，亦不構成任何證券之要約或要約之引誘。報告中之份額變動、營收影響與情境機率係 ToAlpha 依公開資料、供應鏈訪
查與⾃訂假設推算，涉及多項假設，實際結果可能與本報告所述有重⼤差異。本報告所引⽤之資料雖來⾃我們認為可靠之來源，惟 ToAlpha 不保證其
正確性與完整性，資料與觀點如有變動恕不另⾏通知。讀者應⾃⾏判斷並承擔投資⾵險，ToAlpha 對任何⼈依本報告所為之決定不負任何責任。未經

重點摘要
1
分析師／林亞瑱
研究範疇／歐洲總經政策與市場分析
《財政與政治不確定性壓抑估值，歐股延續震盪》
市場對法國財政可持續性以及其可能產生的外溢擔
憂升溫，牽動十月以來歐股走勢震盪，銀行股跌勢
較重，所幸食品飲料與健護等防禦型產業 、能源類
股上揚支撐歐股收紅。評估目前情勢屬法國財政信
心危機，而非歐洲主權風險全面升溫；融資成本上
升對歐洲企業帶來壓力，然整體槓桿可控，財務體
質脆弱的企業面臨較大風險。隨著預算協商進行、
財政擔憂短線難消除，歐央12月利率會議可能再升
息一碼，公債殖利率預估將維持高位，加上中東地
緣風險未除，將對市場情緒與歐股評價帶來壓力 ，
預估歐股短線將延續震盪走勢，維持中立評等。

重點摘要
2
• 法國財政疑慮致法德利差大
幅擴大。
• 預估現任總理勒克努仍有機
會透過援引 《憲法 》第49.3
條於年底前通過一份財政整
頓力度較有限的預算案 ，然
而，即使預算案過關 ，也難

資料來源：野村，2026/10/7，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/10。
2027年預算提案樽節開支，但將面對政治角力
《法國財政疑慮、政治僵局與社會動盪導致法德利
差擴大，明年大選前政治風險溢酬預估維持高檔》
十月份法國公債殖利率大幅上升 ，反映法國政治
局勢分裂 、財政整頓前景不明 ，加上明年總統大
選恐由極右派勝出 ，市場對法國財政可持續性的
疑慮升高 ，法國十年期公債殖利率 10月下旬一度
突破5%，創2002年7月以來新高。

近期法國各地爆發大規模抗議活動 ，亦對市場帶
來衝擊 。法國政治與財政風險升溫 ，反而吸引資
金轉向德國公債避險 ，加上近期金融條件趨緊 、
市場對歐央升息的預期相較九月底降溫 ，推動德
國十年期公債殖利率自高檔回落 ，法德利差大幅
擴大 、10/22一度突破 170個基點 ，創2011年11
月歐債危機以來新高(統計至10/26)。
針對2027年預算 ，法國政府提出 430億歐元財政
整頓方案，目標將財政赤字占 GDP比率自2026年
的5.4%降至2027年的5%，措施包括凍結公部門
薪資、限制每月超過1,260歐元的退休金通膨調整
幅度、延長針對大型企業的特別稅但縮小適用範
圍等。反對黨已對多項措施表達反對 ，若不採取
任何樽節措施 ，2027年法國財政赤字占 GDP比率
預估將達6.5%。由於大選在即 ，從近期極右派國
民聯盟釋出的意向來看 ，雖不支持預算案 ，卻也
不願在大選前引發政治危機 ，預估現任總理勒克
努仍有機會透過援引 《憲法》第49.3條於年底前
通過一份財政整頓力度較有限的預算案 。然而，

即使預算案過關，也難扭轉法國債務攀升趨勢。

重點摘要
3
近來融資成本 上升進一步加重法國的債務負擔 。
信評機構 惠譽(2026/10/12)上調利息支出預測 ，
預估2026年至2028年間，利息支出將累積佔GDP
的0.8%。利息支出占GDP比率預估將由2026年約
2.5%升至 20 28年約3.3%，2030年進一步升至
3.8%，反映高融資成本對財政的壓力將逐步顯現 。
利息支出增加將壓縮其他公共支出及財政空間 。
若赤字改善幅度不如預期 ，債務比率恐持續上升 。
2027年總統大選亦將牽動法國財政政策與債市走
向。法國總統大選預計於 2027年4至5月舉行，第
一輪投票訂於 4/18，若無候選人取得過半選票將
於5/2進行第二輪投票。最新民調顯示，極右派國
民聯盟候選人勒龐在首輪支持度領先 ，超過三成；

極左派法國不屈黨候選人梅朗雄近期支持度上升 ，
引發市場擔憂將發生沒有中間派候選人進入第二
輪決選的不利情境。
梅朗雄主張擴張性財政政策 ，對市場最為不利 ，
勝選機率較高的勒龐 10/6提出財政整頓方案並且
主張建立一個可信的債務穩定框架 ，試圖安撫市
場對法國財政前景的疑慮 。在面對市場壓力下 ，
勒龐表達更具財政紀律的立場有助稍稍緩和財政
擴張的擔憂 ，但其財政整頓方案的政策假設與具
體實施存在不確定性 ，加上大選後很可能緊接著
進行國會改選 ，政治版圖維持分裂狀態不利財政
改革。
資料來源：Fitch，2026/10/12，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/10。
融資成本上升將加重法國債務負擔

重點摘要
4
整體而言 ，財政和政治不確定性預估將令法德利
差維持在高位 ，下檔情境為若 2027年預算案無法
於年底前通過 ，恐將面臨更大的財政惡化與市場
壓力，十年期法德利差恐有進一步擴大的風險。
《目前情勢屬法國財政信心危機，而非歐洲主權風
險全面升溫》
儘管投資人對法國債券要求更高的風險補償 ，但
近期法國公債標售仍有足夠的認購需求 。除此之
外，目前法德利差擴大對整體歐洲市場的外溢影
響尚屬可控 ，多數歐洲國家相對德國利差雖受牽
動近期亦出現較明顯的擴大 ，但利差水準仍可控 。
以政府債務占 GDP比重較高的義大利來看 ，十年
期義德利差 10/22一度升至 124個基點 ，回升至
2025年4月水準，但仍遠低於 2011年歐債危機時

期。因此，目前情勢仍偏向法國自身的財政信心
危機，而非歐洲主權信用風險全面升溫。
資料來源：Bloomberg，2024年來至2026/10/21，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/10。

5
• 10月歐元區 PMI維持擴張但

《歐元區PMI維持擴張但成長放緩，通膨壓力面臨
上行風險》
歐元區10月綜合PMI初值由9月的53.1降至51.9，
雖連續四個月高於 50、顯示經濟持續擴張 ，但成
長動能有所放緩 。法國受到大規模罷工與抗議活
動影響 ，商業活動下滑 ，成為主要拖累因素之一 ，
法國10月綜合PMI初值降至48.4，明顯落後其他
歐元區國家 。另一方面 ，能源及投入成本 上升迫
使企業提高售價，通膨壓力未明顯緩解。
中東緊張局勢延宕，布蘭特原油期貨價格升至116

美元/桶，TTF天然氣期貨價格升至96歐元/兆瓦時，
天然氣價格持續突破今年高點(統計至10/26)。
目前能源價格高於歐洲央行九月的基準與不利情
境預測 ，歐元區通膨面臨上行風險 ，不過未出現
明顯的二輪通膨效應 ，經濟成長則面臨下行壓力 ，
預期歐洲央行連續升息機率低 。此外，近期公債
殖利率上升亦發揮了緊縮效果 ，預估歐洲央行將
暫時按兵不動 。待12月份利率會議 ，屆時歐洲央
行將取得更新的經濟預測 ，可據此重新評估通膨
與成長前景 ，若能源價格及通膨壓力持續偏高 ，
不排除12月將再升息一碼 ，將存款機制利率調升
至2.75%。
針對近期主權債券殖利率大幅走升 ，預估歐洲央
行將密切關注其走勢 ，但長債利率的上升尚未達
到表明主權債券市場面臨顯著壓力的水平 ，應不
足以觸發政策調整。
資料來源：摩根士丹利，2026/10/22，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/10。

重點摘要
7
《融資成本上升對歐洲企業帶來壓力，然整體槓桿
率尚可控，財務體質脆弱的企業面臨較大風險》
主權債券市場的壓力令歐洲企業信用利差同步擴大，
自9月以來，歐洲投資等級債信用利差擴大31個基
點至108個基點，非投資等級債信用利差擴大幅度
較為顯著，擴大105個基點至363個基點，兩者均
回升至2025年5月以來高點(統計至10/26止)。所幸，
歐洲企業資產負債表整體仍維持穩健。儘管淨債務
/EBITDA比率自低檔回升，但整體槓桿水準與歷史
水準相比並不高，仍處健康水準。因此，債務成本
上升雖增加企業的財務負擔，尤其是財務體質較脆
弱的企業面臨較高的風險，但目前尚不至於構成系
統性威脅。另一方面，融資成本上升，企業獲利可

能面臨壓力 ，根據高盛證券 (10/23)預估，在其他
條件不變下，債務成本每上升100個基點，道瓊歐
洲600企業每股盈餘 (EPS)將減少約3%，其中財務
體質較脆弱的企業所受衝擊最大，具備強健資產負
債表與穩定利潤率的企業較能抵禦高利率影響。
《利率維持高位壓抑估值，歐股短線將延續震盪》
隨著預算協商進行、財政擔憂短線難消除，歐央12
月利率會議可能再升息一碼，公債殖利率預估將維
持高位，加上中東地緣風險未除，近期市場對美國
期中選舉後，美伊敵對行動可能重燃的預期升溫，
均將對市場情緒與歐股評價帶來壓力，預估歐股短
線將延續震盪走勢，所幸，LSEG I/B/E/S(10/22)統
計，道瓊歐洲600企業3Q26財報公布雖仍在初期階
段，但目前整體獲利優於財報季前預測，2026年企
業獲利預估相較10月初呈現上修趨勢，歐洲企業獲
利成長應有助提供下檔支撐，歐股維持中立評等。
資料來源：高盛證券，2026/10/23，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10。

《土耳其基金清算風波 、區域雜音增多 ，新歐展

MSCI新興歐洲指數 10月以來下跌 5%，希臘、土
耳其與波蘭股市全數下跌 ，又以希臘與土耳其股

由金融股領跌 ，除了因今年來漲勢不錯引發獲利
了結外 ，法德利差擴大連帶推升希臘與德國公債
殖利率利差 ，市場擔憂法國債市風險蔓延 ，壓抑
銀行股回檔。土耳其股市則受到9月中旬多檔基金
爆發贖回危機所拖累 ，多家土耳其資產管理公司
未能滿足投資人贖回請求 ，土耳其資本市場委員
會下令清算七家資產管理公司管理的 131檔基金 ，
合計資產管理規模約 1兆里拉，MSCI 10/15宣布

凍結土耳其股票的部分指數調整措施 ，並警告若
土耳其未能改善市場誠信與透明度問題 ，最快將
於2027年6月考慮將其從新興市場降級 。考量地
緣風險未除 、土耳其基金清算問題造成的市場動
盪，維持新歐股市中立看法。
貨幣政策方面，9月波蘭CPI年增率升至4.1%，較
8月的3.4%上升且超過央行容忍區間上限 ，波蘭
央行10月利率會議維持政策利率於 3.75%不變。
中東地緣僵局持續 、能源價格維持高檔更久令通
膨前景存在上行風險 ，預估波蘭央行將更長時間
維持利率不變 。土耳其方面 ，10/22土耳其央行
宣布將一週附買回利率調降 100個基點 ，從37%
下調至36%，為時隔9個月再次降息，符合市場預
期，央行會後聲明顯示 ，儘管地緣政治局勢發展
令能源價格維持高檔可能對通膨前景帶來上行風
險，然而，土耳其國內需求疲軟 ，供給面衝擊對
國內價格的傳導效應有限 ，暗示有持續放鬆貨幣
政策限制性的空間。

壓然無礙趨勢
分析師／張志揚

美洲投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/10/30
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
《盤勢看法》
科技類股於10月中旬出現明顯修正 ，不過市場資金
並未明顯撤離 ，而是在其他類股輪動 ，使標普500
指數的修正幅度相對有限 。長期來說，穩健的經濟
基本面才是股市行情延續的關鍵 ，而目前的景氣和
企業獲利支持這樣的趨勢 ，只是高利率環境仍將使
市場資金更加嚴格看待資產的成長性 。拉美方面 ，
現任左翼總統魯拉在 10月初第一輪投票落後的情況
下，於第二輪決選中逆轉 ，市場重新定價財政前景 ，
利率上升壓力將提高 。據此，將拉美和巴西股市的
評等由正向下調為中立。
